+def act_of(relay=RELAY):
+    """the act named by <relay>/data/act_current.json, or None where the file is absent or unreadable."""
+    try:

+def sha256(p):
+    with open(p, 'rb') as f:
+        return hashlib.sha256(f.read()).hexdigest()
+
+
+def instance_record(prev):
+    """(4)(iii)'s instance from the record: the previous act's hook bank, read against the sha256 its act's root names."""
+    root = os.path.join(RELAY, 'data', '%s_act_root.json' % prev)
+    rel = 'data/%s_%s.txt' % (prev, EXCLUDED_KIND)
+    try:
+        rj = json.load(open(root, encoding='utf-8'))
+    except (OSError, ValueError):
+        return dict(named=False, rel=rel, root=root)
+    banks = dict(rj.get('banks') or {})   # a root's banks: its `banks` map, or its `items` rows "data/<name> <sha256>"
+    for it in rj.get('items') or []:
+        parts = it.split(' ')
+        if len(parts) == 2 and parts[0].startswith('data/'):
+            banks.setdefault(parts[0], parts[1])
+    p = os.path.join(RELAY, *rel.split('/'))
+    return dict(named=rel in banks, rel=rel, root=root, rooted=banks.get(rel), now=sha256(p) if os.path.isfile(p) else None)
+
+
+def instance_constructed(act):
+    """(4)(iii)'s constructed instance, in a scratch directory outside data/ and tools/: a test relay holding act_current.json, three
+    banks and a test root naming them by sha256; one refusal sent through refuse() with the standing path, then one through a refuse
+    bound to a rooted bank (the old form). Returns each run's changed root-named banks. Nothing is deleted; the scratch stays."""
+    scratch = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix='standing-hook-control-')
+    d = os.path.join(scratch, 'data')
+    os.makedirs(d)
+    with open(os.path.join(d, 'act_current.json'), 'w', encoding='utf-8', newline='\n') as f:
+        f.write(json.dumps({'act': act}) + '\n')
+    named = ['%s_closing.txt' % act, '%s_defects.txt' % act, '%s_old_hook_bank.txt' % act]
+    for n in named:
+        with open(os.path.join(d, n), 'w', encoding='utf-8', newline='\n') as f:
+            f.write('a test bank %s\n' % n)
+    test_root = {'data/' + n: sha256(os.path.join(d, n)) for n in named}
+
+    def changed():
+        return sorted(r for r, h in test_root.items() if sha256(os.path.join(scratch, *r.split('/'))) != h)
+
+    refuse('PIPE', 'the control`s refused call', 'Bash', {'command': 'git status | head'}, relay=scratch)
+    new = changed()
+    out = refusals_path(scratch)
+    old_path = os.path.join(d, named[2])
+    with open(old_path, 'a', encoding='utf-8', newline='\n') as f:   # the old form: the refusal appended to a bank the root names
+        f.write('a refusal line, the old form\n')
+    old = changed()
+    return dict(scratch=scratch, new_changed=new, new_bank=os.path.relpath(out, scratch).replace(os.sep, '/'),
+                new_bank_named='data/' + os.path.basename(out) in test_root, old_changed=old)

+def seal_record(act, parts, rev, defects_rev, withheld_spec):
+    """the seal's record rebuilt from the banks: the sealed tools' sha256 at the seal's commit `rev` (the seal hashed the working tree, and
+    the seal's commit is the first to carry what it hashed), and apart at the record's own relay head; the letters and the defects' sha256
+    of the defects bank at `defects_rev`; neg_shown's count and the withheld field from the pre-run's exercise banks at `rev`; the runs on
+    the previous act's banks. Returns (the rebuilt fields, the record as written)."""
+    rec = A.jl('%s_seal_hashes.json' % act)
+    head = rec.get('relay_head')
+    tools, at_head = {}, {}
+    for t in rec.get('tools') or {}:
+        b = A.blob(rev, 'tools/' + t)
+        tools[t] = A.sha256_bytes(b) if b is not None else None
+        h = A.blob(head, 'tools/' + t) if head else None
+        at_head[t] = A.sha256_bytes(h) if h is not None else None
+    dtext = A.rd('%s_defects.txt' % act, defects_rev)
+    banks = exercise_banks(act, parts, rev)
+    ns = neg_shown(arms_of(banks, parts), banks, withheld_entries(withheld_spec, rev=rev))
+    prev_run = {}
+    for p in sorted(set(p for _n, p in arms_of(banks, parts))):
+        j = A.jl('%s_prevrun_exercise_%s.json' % (act, p), rev)
+        if j:
+            prev_run[p] = dict((k, len(v)) for k, v in j.items() if k in PREV_KEYS)
+    got = dict(tools=tools, tools_at_head=at_head, letters=letters_of(dtext), defects_sha256=A.sha256_bytes(dtext.encode('utf-8')), neg_shown=len(ns.shown),
+               bad=ns.bad, withheld=[dict(arm=n, reason=w) for n, w in ns.withheld], prev_run=prev_run)
+    return got, rec

+def prev_longest(prev, parts, base='arms_prerun', named=None, read=None):
+    """the previous act's longest pre-run phase over its root-named part banks. `named` ({relay path: sha256}) and `read` (path -> bytes or
+    None) default to the previous act's root and the working tree; a constructed control passes its own."""
+    named = A.root_named(prev) if named is None else named
+
+    def _read(rel):
+        p = os.path.join(A.RELAY, *rel.split('/'))
+        if not os.path.isfile(p):
+            return None
+        with open(p, 'rb') as f:
+            return f.read()
+    read = read or _read
+    rows, refused, longest, got = [], [], 0, set()
+    for p in parts:
+        rel = 'data/%s_%s_%s.txt' % (prev, base, p)
+        b = read(rel)
+        want = named.get(rel)
+        have = A.sha256_bytes(b) if b is not None else None
+        if want is None:
+            refused.append((rel, 'not named by the root'))
+        elif have is None:
+            refused.append((rel, 'absent'))
+        elif have != want:
+            refused.append((rel, 'sha256 %s differs from the root`s %s' % (have[:16], want[:16])))
+        else:
+            phases = [int(x) for x in LONGEST.findall(b.decode('utf-8', 'replace'))]
+            got.add(p)
+            longest = max([longest] + phases)
+        rows.append((rel, (want or '-')[:16], (have or '-')[:16], want is not None and have == want))
+    return dict(longest=longest, parts_read=len(got), rows=rows, refused=refused)

### PAIR tools/standing/scan.py split <- tools/b703_checks.py strip_prose : source 4 lines, copy 22 lines, diff 29 lines

-def strip_prose(t):
-    t = re.sub(r'"""[\s\S]*?"""', '', t)
-    t = re.sub(r"'''[\s\S]*?'''", '', t)
-    return NL.join(l.split('#', 1)[0] for l in t.split(NL))
+def split(text):
+    """(code, prose) of a Python source by its tokens: comments and statement-level strings are prose, blanked in place in the code (the
+    code keeps its own text, so `os.remove` stays one word)."""
+    lines_ = text.split(NL)
+    starts = [0]
+    for l in lines_:
+        starts.append(starts[-1] + len(l) + 1)
+    spans, prose = [], []
+    prev = tokenize.NEWLINE
+    for tok in tokenize.generate_tokens(io.StringIO(text).readline):
+        if tok.type == tokenize.COMMENT or (
+                tok.type == tokenize.STRING and prev in (tokenize.NEWLINE, tokenize.NL, tokenize.INDENT, tokenize.DEDENT, tokenize.ENCODING)):
+            prose.append(tok.string)
+            spans.append((starts[tok.start[0] - 1] + tok.start[1], starts[tok.end[0] - 1] + tok.end[1]))
+        if tok.type not in (tokenize.COMMENT, tokenize.NL):
+            prev = tok.type
+    code = list(text)
+    for a, b in spans:
+        for i in range(a, b):
+            if code[i] != NL:
+                code[i] = ' '
+    return ''.join(code), NL.join(prose)

+# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
+"""b716_carry.py -- THE CARRY BY DIFF, data/b709_rules.txt row (1) ((R319)(2), binding from b709; (R326) "The close runs each step as
+data/b715_toolset_coverage.txt marks it"): a tool carried from the previous act is produced from the previous act's tool by the act names'
+substitution alone and its diff banked; the seat reads the carried tool through the previous act's tool-repairs bank and the spans those diffs
+name, by offset and limit, and not whole.
+
+### `carry <name>`: tools/b715_<name>.py -> tools/b716_<name>.py with b715 -> b716, b714 -> b715, (R325) -> (R326), (R324) -> (R325); the diff
+### banked in data/b716_carry_<name>.txt (autojunk=False opcodes, b697's trap) with the spans data/b715_tool_repairs.txt names for the tool;
+### `prune` removes the previous act's routes over its own objects (banked in data/b716_prune_<name>.txt); `repairdiff` banks the carry-time
+### repairs as a diff against the carried form; `spans` summarises in data/b716_carry_spans.txt. Written by the Write tool at b716 from
+### tools/b715_carry.py (222 lines, read through the Read tool by offset and limit, :1-:200 and :200-:222), the act names shifted by one act;
+### no repair.
+"""
+import difflib
+import io
+import os
+import re
+import sys
+import time
+
+ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
+D, T = os.path.join(ROOT, 'data'), os.path.join(ROOT, 'tools')
+NL = chr(10)
+if hasattr(sys.stdout, 'reconfigure'):
+    sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+
+PREV, ACT = 'b715', 'b716'
+SUBS = (('b715', 'b716'), ('b714', 'b715'), ('R325', 'R326'), ('R324', 'R325'))   # ### b716's carry-time repair: b715's six pairs ran
+#   ### '(R324)' -> '(R325)' before 'R325' -> 'R326', shifting an older ruling twice; the bare pairs in descending order shift each name once
+
+
+def rd(p):
+    return io.open(p, encoding='utf-8', errors='replace').read().replace(chr(13), '') if os.path.exists(p) else ''
+
+
+def utc():
+    return time.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ', time.gmtime())
+
+
+def _membrane(p):
+    """standing clauses v2 row (53): the membrane before a write under relay data/ (tools/act_membrane.py guard_path); the exclusion pattern
+    the record tool's where it imports, none before it is carried."""
+    sys.path.insert(0, T)
+    import act_membrane as MEM
+    try:
+        import b716_record as REC
+        ex = REC.R.ROOT_EXCLUDE
+    except Exception:
+        ex = None
+    MEM.guard_path(ACT, p, ex)
+
+
+def substitute(text):
+    """the act names shifted by one act, in order so that b715 -> b716 runs before b714 -> b715."""
+    out = text
+    for a, b in SUBS:
+        out = out.replace(a, b)
+    return out
+
+
+def udiff(old, new, a_name, b_name):
+    sm = difflib.SequenceMatcher(None, old, new, autojunk=False)
+    L = ['--- ' + a_name, '+++ ' + b_name]
+    for tag, i1, i2, j1, j2 in sm.get_opcodes():
+        if tag == 'equal':
+            continue
+        L.append('@@ -%d,%d +%d,%d @@' % (i1 + 1, i2 - i1, j1 + 1, j2 - j1))
+        for l in old[i1:i2]:
+            L.append('-' + l)
+        for l in new[j1:j2]:
+            L.append('+' + l)
+    return L
+
+
+def repair_spans(name):
+    """the rows of the previous act's tool-repairs bank naming tools/<prev>_<name>.py (the row's line and its first words), and the hunk headers
+    of the previous act's repairdiff bank for the tool (data/<prev>_repairdiff_<name>.txt): [(line, header)]."""
+    out = []
+    for i, l in enumerate(rd(os.path.join(D, '%s_tool_repairs.txt' % PREV)).split(NL), 1):
+        if re.search(r'tools/%s_%s\.py' % (PREV, name), l):
+            out.append((i, l.strip()[:90]))
+    for i, l in enumerate(rd(os.path.join(D, '%s_repairdiff_%s.txt' % (PREV, name))).split(NL), 1):
+        if l.startswith('@@'):
+            out.append((i, l.strip()))
+    return out
+
+
+def carry(name, *a):
+    src = os.path.join(T, '%s_%s.py' % (PREV, name))
+    dst = os.path.join(T, '%s_%s.py' % (ACT, name))
+    old = rd(src)
+    if not old:
+        sys.exit('### tools/%s_%s.py IS NOT THERE -- NOTHING CARRIED' % (PREV, name))
+    if os.path.exists(dst) and 'again' not in a:
+        sys.exit('### tools/%s_%s.py EXISTS -- NOTHING WRITTEN (give `again` to rewrite the carried form)' % (ACT, name))
+    new = substitute(old)
+    ol, nl_ = old.split(NL), new.split(NL)
+    diff = udiff(ol, nl_, 'tools/%s_%s.py' % (PREV, name), 'tools/%s_%s.py' % (ACT, name))
+    spans = repair_spans(name)
+    with open(dst + '.tmp', 'wb') as fh:
+        fh.write(new.encode('utf-8'))
+    os.replace(dst + '.tmp', dst)
+    changed = sum(1 for l in diff if l.startswith('+') and not l.startswith('+++'))
+    L = ['%s -- THE CARRY BY DIFF (data/b709_rules.txt row (1)): tools/%s_%s.py -> tools/%s_%s.py (%s)' % (ACT, PREV, name, ACT, name, utc()), '',
+         '### the previous act`s tool carried by the act names` substitution alone (%s); %d lines, %d lines changed by the substitution; the seat reads' % (
+             ', '.join('%s -> %s' % s for s in SUBS), len(nl_), changed),
+         '### the carried tool through the spans the previous act`s banks name for it (below) and this diff, by offset and limit, not whole.',
+         '### THE SPANS data/%s_tool_repairs.txt AND data/%s_repairdiff_%s.txt NAME FOR THIS TOOL (the bank`s line ; the row or hunk header):' % (PREV, PREV, name)]
+    L += ['  :%d ; %s' % s for s in spans] or ['  NONE: no repair of this tool at %s; the seat reads this diff`s hunks alone' % PREV]
+    L += ['', '### THE DIFF OF THE SUBSTITUTION, FROM autojunk=False OPCODES:'] + ['  ' + l for l in diff]
+    L += ['', '### ### **CARRIED tools/%s_%s.py ; LINES %d ; CHANGED BY THE SUBSTITUTION %d ; REPAIR SPANS AT %s %d ; THE SEAT READS THE SPANS, NOT THE WHOLE.**' % (
+        ACT, name, len(nl_), changed, PREV, len(spans))]
+    p = os.path.join(D, '%s_carry_%s.txt' % (ACT, name))
+    _membrane(p)
+    with open(p + '.tmp', 'wb') as fh:
+        fh.write((NL.join(L) + NL).encode('utf-8'))
+    os.replace(p + '.tmp', p)
+    print(NL.join(L[:6] + L[-1:]))
+
+
+def prune(name, start, end, *a):
+    """`prune <name> <start prefix> <end prefix>`: the lines of tools/b716_<name>.py from the one line starting with <start prefix> up to (not
+    including) the one line starting with <end prefix> removed, the removal banked as a hunk in data/b716_prune_<name>.txt (appended)."""
+    dst = os.path.join(T, '%s_%s.py' % (ACT, name))
+    L = rd(dst).split(NL)
+    si = [i for i, l in enumerate(L) if l.startswith(start)]
+    ei = [i for i, l in enumerate(L) if l.startswith(end)]
+    if len(si) != 1 or len(ei) != 1 or ei[0] <= si[0]:
+        sys.exit('### THE PRUNE MARKERS DO NOT NAME ONE SPAN (start %s, end %s) -- NOTHING WRITTEN' % (si, ei))
+    s, e = si[0], ei[0]
+    new = L[:s] + L[e:]
+    with open(dst + '.tmp', 'wb') as fh:
+        fh.write(NL.join(new).encode('utf-8'))
+    os.replace(dst + '.tmp', dst)
+    hunk = ['@@ -%d,%d +%d,0 @@ PRUNED (%s)' % (s + 1, e - s, s + 1, utc())] + ['-' + l for l in L[s:e]]
+    p = os.path.join(D, '%s_prune_%s.txt' % (ACT, name))
+    _membrane(p)
+    prev = rd(p)
+    head = '' if prev else ('%s -- THE PRUNES OF THE CARRIED tools/%s_%s.py: the previous act`s routes over its own objects, removed at carry time (data/b709_rules.txt row (1)); rows are hunks.' % (ACT, ACT, name) + NL + NL)
+    with open(p + '.tmp', 'wb') as fh:
+        fh.write((prev + head + NL.join(hunk) + NL + NL).encode('utf-8'))
+    os.replace(p + '.tmp', p)
+    print('  pruned tools/%s_%s.py :%d-:%d (%d lines, %r .. %r) ; banked in data/%s_prune_%s.txt' % (ACT, name, s + 1, e, e - s, start[:40], end[:40], ACT, name))
+
+
+def repairdiff(name, *a):
+    """`repairdiff <name>`: the carry-time repairs of tools/b716_<name>.py as a diff against its carried form; a pruned span printed as its hunk
+    header with its line count, every other change in full; written to data/b716_repairdiff_<name>.txt."""
+    old = substitute(rd(os.path.join(T, '%s_%s.py' % (PREV, name)))).split(NL)
+    new = rd(os.path.join(T, '%s_%s.py' % (ACT, name))).split(NL)
+    sm = difflib.SequenceMatcher(None, old, new, autojunk=False)
+    L = ['%s -- THE CARRY-TIME REPAIRS OF tools/%s_%s.py AS A DIFF: the carried form (tools/%s_%s.py with the act names substituted) against the file now (%s)' % (ACT, ACT, name, PREV, name, utc()), '',
+         '--- tools/%s_%s.py (the carried form)' % (ACT, name), '+++ tools/%s_%s.py (now)' % (ACT, name)]
+    n_del, n_ins, n_hunks = 0, 0, 0
+    for tag, i1, i2, j1, j2 in sm.get_opcodes():
+        if tag == 'equal':
+            continue
+        n_hunks += 1
+        L.append('@@ -%d,%d +%d,%d @@' % (i1 + 1, i2 - i1, j1 + 1, j2 - j1))
+        if tag == 'delete' and i2 - i1 > 12:
+            L.append('-  ### %d lines pruned (data/%s_prune_%s.txt), the first: %s' % (i2 - i1, ACT, name, old[i1][:100]))
+            n_del += i2 - i1
+            continue
+        for l in old[i1:i2]:
+            L.append('-' + l)
+        for l in new[j1:j2]:
+            L.append('+' + l)
+        n_del += i2 - i1
+        n_ins += j2 - j1
+    L += ['', '### ### **HUNKS %d ; LINES REMOVED %d ; LINES ADDED %d.**' % (n_hunks, n_del, n_ins)]
+    p = os.path.join(D, '%s_repairdiff_%s.txt' % (ACT, name))
+    _membrane(p)
+    with open(p + '.tmp', 'wb') as fh:
+        fh.write((NL.join(L) + NL).encode('utf-8'))
+    os.replace(p + '.tmp', p)
+    print(NL.join(L[-1:]))
+
+
+def spans(*a):
+    names = [f[len(ACT) + 1:-3] for f in sorted(os.listdir(T)) if f.startswith(ACT + '_') and f.endswith('.py') and os.path.exists(os.path.join(D, '%s_carry_%s.txt' % (ACT, f[len(ACT) + 1:-3])))]
+    L = ['%s -- THE CARRIED TOOLS AND THEIR READ SPANS (N3 of b709; data/b709_rules.txt row (1)) (%s)' % (ACT, utc()), '',
+         '### rows are carried tools: the tool | the spans the previous act`s banks name | the substitution`s hunks (count) | the bank.']
+    tot = 0
+    for n in names:
+        sp = repair_spans(n)
+        bank = rd(os.path.join(D, '%s_carry_%s.txt' % (ACT, n)))
+        hunks = sum(1 for l in bank.split(NL) if l.strip().startswith('@@'))
+        tot += len(sp)
+        L.append('  tools/%s_%s.py | spans %d | substitution hunks %d | data/%s_carry_%s.txt' % (ACT, n, len(sp), hunks, ACT, n))
+    L += ['', '### ### **CARRIED TOOLS %d ; SPANS NAMED %d ; EACH CARRIED BY THE SUBSTITUTION AND BANKED BY ITS DIFF.**' % (len(names), tot)]
+    p = os.path.join(D, '%s_carry_spans.txt' % ACT)
+    _membrane(p)
+    with open(p + '.tmp', 'wb') as fh:
+        fh.write((NL.join(L) + NL).encode('utf-8'))
+    os.replace(p + '.tmp', p)
+    print(NL.join(L))
+
+
+def spanlist(name, *a):
+    """`spanlist <name>`: the new-side line ranges of the previous act's repairdiff bank for the tool (data/b715_repairdiff_<name>.txt, its hunk
+    headers `@@ -i,n +j,m @@`), merged where they touch within 3 lines -- the spans the carry reads in the carried file (the substitution keeps
+    every line in place, so the previous act's new side is the carried file's numbering before this act's own repairs). Print only."""
+    hs = []
+    for l in rd(os.path.join(D, '%s_repairdiff_%s.txt' % (PREV, name))).split(NL):
+        m = re.match(r'^@@ -\d+,\d+ \+(\d+),(\d+) @@', l)
+        if m and int(m.group(2)) > 0:
+            hs.append([int(m.group(1)), int(m.group(1)) + int(m.group(2)) - 1])
+    merged = []
+    for s, e in sorted(hs):
+        if merged and s <= merged[-1][1] + 3:
+            merged[-1][1] = max(merged[-1][1], e)
+        else:
+            merged.append([s, e])
+    print('  %s: hunks %d ; spans %d ; lines %d' % (name, len(hs), len(merged), sum(e - s + 1 for s, e in merged)))
+    print('  ' + ' '.join(':%d-:%d' % (s, e) for s, e in merged))
+
+
+if __name__ == '__main__':
+    fn = {'carry': carry, 'spans': spans, 'prune': prune, 'repairdiff': repairdiff, 'spanlist': spanlist}.get(sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else '')
+    if fn is None:
+        print('usage: b716_carry.py carry <name> [again] | prune <name> <start prefix> <end prefix> | repairdiff <name> | spans')
+        sys.exit(2)
+    fn(*sys.argv[2:])

+# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
+"""b716_citation.py -- THE CITATION CHECK AS A GATE STEP (standing from b662, OPEN_TRAILS :14136), b716's step zero, run before any other step
+(the ferry's Component 0); the ferry banked before it by the Write tool; and, from (R278)(4) (OPEN_TRAILS :14292), THE CHECK ON EACH MID-ACT
+AUTHOR ANSWER before the seat acts on it.
+### The helpers are b677's, IMPORTED from tools/b677_citation.py and unchanged, its anchor pointed at this act's ruling and its session at this
+### act's (bde68a30, a new session after the clear; the anchor (R326)'s line); the answer route is b682's, imported from
+### tools/b682_citation.py with its act names pointed at b716, b706's repair carried (a bare eight-hex token a bank names as a sha256 or a
+### kernel's lakefile pins as a rev is not read as a commit); the `word` route is b694's. Routes: `check [dry]` -> data/b716_citation_check.txt
+### (.json) ; `ferry` -> data/b716_ferry_scan.txt ; `answer <needle>` / `word <needle>` -> the check on one answer.
+### Carried by diff from b715_citation.py (data/b716_carry_citation.txt), b715's cite rows pruned (data/b716_prune_citation.txt) and this
+### ruling's written by the Edit tool; this tool writes only the banks named above. Its (R326)(1) row reads lv's release list by `gh` (the
+### seat's own authenticated client; the repository's owner path its only argument, no identifier of the author's person). A kernel's file at a tag is read here by `git cat-file` and `git grep` inside this
+### tool (a named tool's read, its rows banked), never printed whole.
+"""
+import glob
+import json
+import os
+import re
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import time
+
+ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
+D, T = os.path.join(ROOT, 'data'), os.path.join(ROOT, 'tools')
+sys.path.insert(0, T)
+import b677_citation as C   # noqa: E402  ### the helpers, imported unchanged
+import b716_worklist as K   # noqa: E402
+import act_membrane as MEM  # noqa: E402  ### (R326)(2)(ii): the membrane before every bank write of this tool
+
+
+def _root_exclude():
+    import b716_record as REC   # ### the record tool's exclusion pattern, the one the root route reads (imported at the call, nothing written)
+    return REC.R.ROOT_EXCLUDE
+

+C.SESSION = 'C:/Users/echo chamber/.claude/projects/D--/%s.jsonl' % K.SESSION_ID
+
+

+    """the act's continuation sessions (K.SESSIONS_AFTER, b671's form) as transcript paths, read by b677's helpers after the seat's session."""

+C.SESSIONS_AFTER = continuation_sessions()
+NL = chr(10)
+PP = C.PP
+C.ANCHOR, C.TAIL = K.ANCHOR, K.TAIL

+g, rd = C.g, C.rd
+if hasattr(sys.stdout, 'reconfigure'):

+
+def act_root_line(act):
+    t = rd(os.path.join(D, 'act_roots.txt')) or ''
+    return [l.split() for l in t.split(NL) if l.strip() and l.split()[0] == act]
+
+
+def lines_of(path):
+    return (rd(path) or '').split(NL)
+
+
+def first(L, needle, start=1):
+    return next((i + 1 for i, l in enumerate(L) if i + 1 >= start and needle in l), 0)
+
+
+def at(L, n):
+    return L[n - 1] if n and len(L) >= n else ''
+
+
+def _ws(s):
+    return ''.join((s or '').split())
+
+
+def _jl(name):
+    try:
+        return json.loads(rd(os.path.join(D, name)) or '{}')
+    except Exception:
+        return {}
+
+
+def _jl_any(path):
+    """a JSON file by its full path (the mirror's roster under tools/), {} where absent or unreadable."""
+    try:
+        return json.loads(rd(path) or '{}')
+    except Exception:
+        return {}
+
+
+def _blob(repo, rev, path):
+    """a file's text at a revision, read inside the tool (`git cat-file -p`), '' when absent."""
+    r = subprocess.run(['git', '-C', repo, 'cat-file', '-p', '%s:%s' % (rev, path)], capture_output=True)
+    return r.stdout.decode('utf-8', 'replace') if r.returncode == 0 else ''
+
+
+def _decl_files(repo, rev, name):
+    """the .lean files at `rev` that DECLARE `name` (its last dotted component after theorem/lemma/def/structure), by git grep -l inside the tool."""
+    short = name.split('.')[-1]
+    r = subprocess.run(['git', '-C', repo, 'grep', '-l', '-E', r'^\s*(private\s+)?(theorem|lemma|def|structure|abbrev|instance)\s+' + re.escape(short) + r'(\s|$|:)',
+                        rev, '--', '*.lean'], capture_output=True)
+    return [l.split(':', 1)[1] for l in r.stdout.decode('utf-8', 'replace').split(NL) if ':' in l]
+
+
+def _decl_line(text, short):
+    rx = re.compile(r'^\s*(private\s+)?(theorem|lemma|def|structure|abbrev|instance)\s+' + re.escape(short) + r'(\s|$|:)')
+    return next((i + 1 for i, l in enumerate(text.split(NL)) if rx.match(l)), 0)
+
+
+def _remote_heads(repo):
+    out = g(repo, 'ls-remote', '--heads', 'origin')[1] or ''
+    return dict((l.split('\t', 1)[1].strip(), l.split('\t', 1)[0]) for l in out.split(NL) if '\t' in l)
+
+
+def _quote(text, start, stop):
+    """the ruling's quotation that opens with `start`, cut at `stop` (the quotation's own closing mark and the clause after it)."""
+    i = text.find(start)
+    if i < 0:
+        return ''
+    j = text.find(stop, i)
+    return text[i:j] if j > 0 else ''
+
+
+def rows():
+    """(where, what, ok, how, component)."""
+    R = []
+    add = lambda where, what, ok, how, comp: R.append((where, what, bool(ok), how, comp))   # noqa: E731
+    p = lambda *a: os.path.join(ROOT, *a)   # noqa: E731
+    OT = os.path.join(PP, 'OPEN_TRAILS.md')
+    FI = os.path.join(PP, 'FINDINGS.md')

+        os.path.exists(os.path.join(PP, 'phase2', 'method', 'THE_KEYSTONE_CENSUS_v0_8_2.md')), 'exists at the working tree', '0')

+        '(R324)(7)' in at(law, 1) and 'TWO FORMS' in at(law, 66), ':1 %r ; :66 %r' % (at(law, 1)[:40], at(law, 66).strip()[:40]), '0')

+        'quote %d characters, the line %d ; equal byte for byte %s' % (len(q), len(cl[0]), q == cl[0]), '1')

+    clo_s = (g(ROOT, 'log', '-1', '--format=%H %s', 'cf51ee95')[1] or '').strip()
+    rmain = (g(ROOT, 'ls-remote', 'origin', 'refs/heads/main')[1] or '').split()
+    add('ruling (1)', 'its relay commit the record`s (fdddea13, "b715 record"), the closing`s cf51ee95 ("b715: the closing"), the remote main',
+        rec_s[41:].startswith('b715 record') and clo_s[41:].startswith('b715: the closing') and rmain[:1] == [clo_s[:40]],
+        '%s ; %s ; remote main %s' % (rec_s[:52], clo_s[:58], (rmain or ['NONE'])[0][:8]), '1')
+    sdir = p('tools', 'standing')
+    sfiles = sorted(f for f in os.listdir(sdir) if os.path.isfile(os.path.join(sdir, f))) if os.path.isdir(sdir) else []
+    scan = rd(p('data', 'b715_standing_scan.txt')) or ''
+    add('ruling (1)', 'tools/standing/ at v1.0.0, 11 files, act literals in the code 0 (VERSION ; data/b715_standing_scan.txt)',

+        'VERSION %r ; files %d ; the scan`s figure %s' % ((rd(os.path.join(sdir, 'VERSION')) or '').strip(), len(sfiles), 'LITERALS IN THE CODE 0' in scan), '1')
+    cov = lines_of(p('data', 'b715_toolset_coverage.txt'))
+    add('ruling (1)', 'the coverage table at 1 of 25 STANDING ([18]), the core inside [3], [7], [8], [17], [20], [25] (data/b715_toolset_coverage.txt :36)',

+def standing_rows(text, add):
+    """the citation tool reads the standing clauses by version (data/b712_rules.txt row (1)): for each version a ferry names as "standing clauses v<N>"
+    outside a quotation, the bank data/standing_clauses_v<N>.txt present with its VERSION line -- v1 with its control banked AS WANTED; a version this
+    act writes at step zero (v2, (R326)(2)) read as TO BE WRITTEN when absent and from the bank when present."""
+    import b715_standing as SC
+    flat = re.sub(r'“[^”]*”', '', ' '.join(text.split()))
+    names = sorted(set(int(m.group(1)) for m in re.finditer(r'standing clauses(?:’)? v(\d+)', flat, re.I)))
+    for v in names or [1]:
+        p = os.path.join(D, 'standing_clauses_v%d.txt' % v)
+        t = rd(p) or ''
+        ver = re.search(r'^VERSION: (\d+)$', t, re.M)
+        if v == 1:
+            ctl = _jl('b715_standing_control.json')
+            add('ferry (standing clauses)', 'the standing clauses v1: data/standing_clauses_v1.txt', bool(t) and bool(ver) and ver.group(1) == '1' and bool(SC.parse(t)) and ctl.get('ok') is True,
+                'present %s ; VERSION %s ; rows %d ; the re-assembly control AS WANTED %s' % (bool(t), ver.group(1) if ver else 'NONE', len(SC.parse(t)), ctl.get('ok')), '0')
+        elif not t:
+            add('ferry (standing clauses)', 'the standing clauses v%d the ferry names: data/standing_clauses_v%d.txt -- TO BE WRITTEN at step zero ((R326)(2))' % (v, v), True, 'absent, as ordered', '0')
+        else:
+            add('ferry (standing clauses)', 'the standing clauses v%d: data/standing_clauses_v%d.txt' % (v, v), bool(ver) and ver.group(1) == str(v), 'present ; VERSION %s' % (ver.group(1) if ver else 'NONE'), '0')
+
+
+def _write(L, R, miss):
+    for n_ in ('b716_citation_check.txt', 'b716_citation_check.json'):
+        MEM.guard('b716', n_, _root_exclude())
+    for name, data in (('b716_citation_check.txt', (NL.join(L) + NL).encode('utf-8')),
+                       ('b716_citation_check.json', (json.dumps(dict(at=time.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ', time.gmtime()), rows=R,
+                                                                       misses=[r[1] for r in miss]), indent=1, ensure_ascii=False) + NL).encode('utf-8'))):
+        q = os.path.join(D, name)
+        with open(q + '.tmp', 'wb') as fh:
+            fh.write(data)
+        os.replace(q + '.tmp', q)
+
+
+def check(*a):
+    text = C.ferry_text()
+    R = rows()
+    miss = [r for r in R if not r[2]]
+    L = ['b716 -- THE CITATION CHECK, A GATE STEP AT STEP ZERO, BEFORE ANY OTHER STEP (standing from b662; the ferry`s Component 0) (%s)' % time.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ', time.gmtime()), '',
+         '### the source: the ruling and the ferry as pasted, read from the session transcript (%d characters, %r to %r); every cite checked at '
+         'HEAD; rows are cites: where | what | OK or MISS | how | the component that cites it.' % (len(text), ANCHOR, TAIL), '']
+    for where, what, ok, how, comp in R:
+        L.append('  %-4s | %s | %s | %s | Component %s' % ('OK' if ok else 'MISS', where, what, how, comp))
+    L += ['', '### THE MISSES, EACH RAISED TO THE AUTHOR OR ENTERED BY THE FACT CLAUSE BEFORE THE COMPONENT THAT CITES IT RUNS (none repaired silently):']
+    L += ['  %s -- %s (Component %s)' % (what, how, comp) for _w, what, ok, how, comp in miss] or ['  NONE']
+    L += ['', '### ### **CITES CHECKED %d ; OK %d ; MISSES %d.**' % (len(R), len(R) - len(miss), len(miss))]
+    if 'dry' in a:
+        print(NL.join(L))
+        return
+    _write(L, R, miss)
+    print(NL.join(L[-(len(miss) + 4):]))
+
+
+def ferry(*a):
+    """the ferry is banked by the Write tool (data/b716_ferry.txt); this route compares it with the transcript's text and runs the scan."""
+    text = C.ferry_text()
+    q = os.path.join(D, 'b716_ferry.txt')
+    banked = (rd(q) or '').rstrip(NL)
+    r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, '-X', 'utf8', os.path.join(T, 'ferry_scan.py'), q], capture_output=True, cwd=ROOT)
+    s = os.path.join(D, 'b716_ferry_scan.txt')
+    MEM.guard('b716', 'b716_ferry_scan.txt', _root_exclude())
+    with open(s + '.tmp', 'wb') as fh:
+        fh.write((r.stdout + r.stderr).replace(b'\r\n', b'\n'))
+    os.replace(s + '.tmp', s)
+    eq = banked == text.rstrip(NL)
+    print('  b716_ferry.txt %d characters ; the transcript`s text %d characters ; equal %s ; ferry_scan exit %d' % (
+        len(banked), len(text), eq, r.returncode))
+    if not eq:
+        i = next((k for k in range(min(len(banked), len(text))) if banked[k] != text[k]), min(len(banked), len(text)))
+        print('  the first difference at %d: banked %r ; transcript %r' % (i, banked[i - 20:i + 40], text[i - 20:i + 40]))
+
+
+# ### b706's repair of the answer checker (b705 defect (n)), carried: a token that a bank's row names as a sha256 -- the root list, a hashes
+# ### bank's row, a bank line carrying "sha256" -- or that a kernel's lakefile or manifest pins as a rev, is not a commit.
+_HEX8 = re.compile(r'(?<![0-9a-f])([0-9a-f]{8})(?![0-9a-f])')
+
+
+def _sha_rows():
+    out = {}
+    for q in [os.path.join(D, 'act_roots.txt')] + sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(D, '*.txt')), reverse=True):   # ### the later act's row named first
+        t = rd(q) or ''
+        if 'sha256' not in t and not q.endswith('act_roots.txt') and not q.endswith('_hashes.txt'):
+            continue
+        for i, l in enumerate(t.split(NL), 1):
+            if 'sha256' in l or q.endswith('act_roots.txt') or q.endswith('_hashes.txt'):
+                for m in re.finditer(r'(?<![0-9a-f])([0-9a-f]{64})(?![0-9a-f])', l):
+                    out.setdefault(m.group(1)[:8], ('data/' + os.path.basename(q), i))
+    for kd in sorted(d_ for d_ in os.listdir('D:/') if d_.startswith('SIDE-') and os.path.isdir(os.path.join('D:/', d_, '.git'))):
+        for f in ('lakefile.toml', 'lakefile.lean', 'lake-manifest.json'):
+            t = rd(os.path.join('D:/', kd, f)) or ''
+            for i, l in enumerate(t.split(NL), 1):
+                for m in re.finditer(r'(?:rev\s*=\s*"|"rev"\s*:\s*"|@\s*"|rev\s+")([0-9a-f]{40})"', l):
+                    out.setdefault(m.group(1)[:8], ('%s/%s' % (kd, f), i))
+    return out
+
+
+def answer(needle, *a):
+    """(R278)(4): b682's route, imported, with its act names pointed at b716, and b706's repair carried."""
+    import b682_citation as P
+    P.C.ANCHOR = P.ANCHOR = ANCHOR
+    P.C.SESSION = C.SESSION
+    P.C.SESSIONS_AFTER = C.SESSIONS_AFTER
+    P.REPOS = tuple(P.REPOS) + tuple(r for r in (K.EF_DIR, K.LV_DIR, K.JOIN_DIR, 'D:/SIDE-archimedean', 'D:/SIDE-simplicity', 'D:/SIDE-substrate-cluster',
+                                                 'D:/SIDE-window', 'D:/SIDE-effects', 'D:/SIDE-bijection', 'D:/SIDE-interfaces', 'D:/SIDE-li-map',
+                                                 'D:/SIDE-kernel') if r not in P.REPOS and os.path.isdir(r))
+    P.act_root_line, P.D = act_root_line, D
+    P.after_root = lambda: bool(act_root_line('b716'))
+    shas = _sha_rows()
+    base = P.C._user_texts
+
+    def masked(*k, **kw):
+        out = []
+        for t in base(*k, **kw):
+            if t and needle in t:
+                for m in _HEX8.finditer(t):
+                    h8 = m.group(1)
+                    if h8 in shas:
+                        print('  ### SHA256 PREFIX %s... NAMED BY %s :%d -- a root or file hash by its bank`s row, not a commit; OK, masked from the commit reading' % (h8, shas[h8][0], shas[h8][1]))
+                        t = t.replace(h8, 'SHA-' + h8[:4] + '-' + h8[4:])
+            out.append(t)
+        return out
+
+    P.C._user_texts = masked
+    src = open(os.path.join(T, 'b682_citation.py'), encoding='utf-8').read()
+    body = src[src.index('def answer(needle, *a):'):src.index("if __name__ == '__main__':")].replace('b682', 'b716')
+    # ### (R326)(2), the item repair: THE WHOLE ANSWER. b682's cut ends at the first "END" after the needle (`best.find('END', k)`, :221), which a
+    # ### word carrying END (AMENDMENT, APPENDED, RECOMMENDED) ends inside: b715's answers read 468 characters. The cut now ends at the answer's
+    # ### last END (`best.rfind('END')`), the paste's own tail; the substitution printed, the route refusing where it does not apply.
+    if body.count("best.find('END', k)") != 1:
+        sys.exit('### b682`s CUT NOT FOUND ONCE IN ITS answer -- NOTHING CHECKED')
+    body = body.replace("best.find('END', k)", "best.rfind('END')")
+    body = body.replace("    open(q + '.tmp', 'wb')", "    MEM.guard('b716', os.path.basename(q), ROOT_EXCLUDE)\n    open(q + '.tmp', 'wb')")
+    ns = dict(vars(P))
+    ns.update(MEM=MEM, ROOT_EXCLUDE=_root_exclude())
+    exec(compile(body, 'b682_citation.answer (b716)', 'exec'), ns)
+    ns['answer'](needle)
+
+
+def replay(needle, session_id, anchor, *a):
+    """`replay <needle> <session id> <anchor>`: the repair's control, print only -- b682's selection (:210-:219, the latest user text after the
+    anchor carrying the needle and an answer's mark) over the named session, then its cut two ways: b682's (`best.find('END', k)`) and the
+    repaired (`best.rfind('END')`); each cut's length and its last 60 characters printed; nothing written."""
+    import b682_citation as P
+    P.C.SESSION = 'C:/Users/echo chamber/.claude/projects/D--/%s.jsonl' % session_id
+    P.C.SESSIONS_AFTER = ()
+    best, seen = None, False
+    for t in P.C._user_texts():
+        if anchor in t:
+            seen = True
+        u = t.replace('\u2019', "'")
+        if seen and needle in t and ("NAVIGATOR'S ANSWER" in u or "NAVIGATOR'S NOTE" in u or "AUTHOR'S" in u or 'The user answered' in u
+                                     or 'user has answered' in u):
+            best = t
+    if best is None:
+        print('  ### NO ANSWER CARRYING %r AFTER %r IN SESSION %s' % (needle, anchor, session_id))
+        return
+    k = best.find(needle)
+    out = {}
+    for label, e0 in (('b682', best.find('END', k)), ('repaired', best.rfind('END'))):
+        b0 = best.rfind('BEGIN', 0, k)
+        cut = best[b0:e0 + 3] if b0 >= 0 and e0 > k else best
+        out[label] = cut
+        print('  %-8s cut: %d characters ; ends %r' % (label, len(cut), cut[-60:]))
+    print('  the whole answer %d characters ; the repaired cut equal to the text from its BEGIN to its last END: %s' % (
+        len(best), out['repaired'] == best[best.rfind('BEGIN', 0, k):best.rfind('END') + 3]))
+    return dict((k_, len(v)) for k_, v in out.items())
+
+
+def word(needle, *a):
+    """(R278)(4) on an author's word delivered as a bare BEGIN ... END paste: b694's route."""
+    import b682_citation as P
+    base = C._user_texts
+
+    def marked(*k, **kw):
+        return [("THE AUTHOR'S WORD " + t) if (t or '').lstrip().startswith(('BEGIN', '<pasted_content')) and 'END' in (t or '') else t
+                for t in base(*k, **kw)]
+
+    C._user_texts = marked
+    P.C._user_texts = marked
+    answer(needle)
+
+
+if __name__ == '__main__':
+    fn = {'check': check, 'ferry': ferry, 'answer': answer, 'word': word, 'replay': replay}.get(sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else '')
+    if fn is None:
+        print('usage: b716_citation.py check [dry] | ferry | answer <needle> | word <needle> | replay <needle> <session id> <anchor>')
+        sys.exit(2)
+    fn(*sys.argv[2:])

+def _flat(t):
+    return re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', t)
+
+
+def _corrigenda_rows():
+    """the corrigenda bank's rows by number, each row's lines joined and its whitespace collapsed."""
+    rows, cur = {}, None
+    for l in C78.rd(os.path.join(D, 'b675_corrigenda.txt')).replace(chr(13), '').split(NL):
+        m = re.match(r'^  \((\d+)\) ', l)
+        if m:
+            cur = int(m.group(1))
+            rows[cur] = l
+        elif cur is not None and l.startswith('      '):
+            rows[cur] += ' ' + l.strip()
+        elif not l.strip() or l.startswith('###'):
+            cur = None
+    return dict((k, _flat(v)) for k, v in rows.items())
+
+
+def _col_ok(c, cr):
+    if not c:
+        return True
+    _act, _letter, src, where, needle = c
+    if src == 'row':
+        return _flat(needle) in cr.get(where, '')
+    L = C78.rd(os.path.join(ROOT, src)).replace(chr(13), '').split(NL)
+    return len(L) >= where and needle in L[where - 1]
+
+
+def _refused(heads):
+    return [x for x in PREV_LETTERS if 'REFUSED' in heads.get(x, '')]
+
+
+def compose():
+    ev = C78.rd(os.path.join(D, 'b715_events.txt')).replace(chr(13), '').split(NL)
+    starts = [i for i, l in enumerate(ev, 1) if re.match(r'^\(\d+\) ', l)]
+    want_starts = sorted([x[0] for x in LETTERED] + [x[0] for x in RECORDS])
+    heads = dict((n, re.sub(r'^\(\d+\) ', '', ev[n - 1])) for n in starts)
+    miss_heads = [x[0] for x in LETTERED if not heads.get(x[0], '').startswith(x[1])] + \
+                 [x[0] for x in RECORDS if not heads.get(x[0], '').startswith(x[1])]
+    dt = C78.rd(os.path.join(D, 'b715_defects.txt'))
+    rows = REC.letters_of(dt)
+    prev = [re.match(r"\(([a-z]'?)\)", x).group(1) for x in rows]
+    dheads = dict((re.match(r"\(([a-z]'?)\)", x).group(1), x) for x in rows)
+    miss_d = [d[0] for d in DEFECTS if d[1] not in dheads.get(d[0], '')]
+    refused = _refused(dheads)
+    letters = AL.after(prev, len(LETTERED)) if prev == PREV_LETTERS else []
+    cr = _corrigenda_rows()
+    cols = [x[5] for x in DEFECTS] + [x[5] for x in LETTERED] + [x[3] for x in RECORDS]
+    bad_cols = [(c[0], c[1], c[2], c[3]) for c in cols if not _col_ok(c, cr)]
+    f16 = C78.rd(os.path.join(D, 'b716_ferry.txt')).split(NL)
+    ferry_ok = [k for k, n in FERRY_LINES.items() if not (len(f16) >= n and f16[n - 1].startswith(FERRY_NEEDLES[k]))]
+    s = dict(starts=starts, heads=heads, prev=prev, letters=letters, refused=refused, corrigenda_last=max(cr) if cr else 0)
+    bad = [k for k, v in (('the eleven starting lines', starts == want_starts), ('each event`s head', not miss_heads),
+                          ('b715`s seventeen letters', prev == PREV_LETTERS), ('each defect`s head', not miss_d),
+                          ('the six refused by the hook', refused == REFUSED_WANT), ('the nine letters after them', letters == list('rstuvwxyz')),
+                          ('each REPEAT`s line or row and needle', not bad_cols), ('each repair`s ferry line', not ferry_ok),
+                          ('the corrigenda bank`s last row (%d)' % CORRIGENDA_LAST, max(cr) == CORRIGENDA_LAST if cr else False)) if not v]
+    if bad:
+        sys.exit('### THE BANKS NOT AS READ: %s -- NOTHING COMPOSED (event heads missed %s ; defect heads missed %s ; refused %s ; columns %s ; '
+                 'starts %s ; ferry %s)' % (bad, miss_heads, miss_d, refused, bad_cols, starts, ferry_ok))
+    return s
+
+
+def _column(c):
+    if not c:
+        return 'NEW (no row of the evidence set carries its class)'
+    act, letter, src, where, _n = c
+    what = ('%s (%s)' % (act, AL.canon(letter))) if re.match(r"^[a-z]'?$", letter) else ('%s, %s' % (act, letter))
+    place = ('relay data/b675_corrigenda.txt row (%d)' % where) if src == 'row' else ('relay %s :%d' % (src, where))
+    return 'REPEAT of %s, %s' % (what, place)
+
+
+def fraction():
+    """(REPEAT, all) with the refused; (REPEAT, all) without them; (REPEAT, all) among the lettered (b715's defects and the events lettered)."""
+    items = [(d[0] in REFUSED_WANT, bool(d[5]), True) for d in DEFECTS] + [(False, bool(x[5]), True) for x in LETTERED] + \
+            [(False, bool(x[3]), False) for x in RECORDS]
+    ra, na = sum(1 for _r, c, _l in items if c), len(items)
+    rw, nw = sum(1 for r, c, _l in items if c and not r), sum(1 for r, _c, _l in items if not r)
+    rl, nl = sum(1 for _r, c, l in items if c and l), sum(1 for _r, _c, l in items if l)
+    return ra, na, rw, nw, rl, nl
+
+
+def fraction_words():
+    ra, na, rw, nw, rl, nl = fraction()
+    return ('%d of %d (%.2f) with the six refused by the hook ; %d of %d (%.2f) without them ; among the lettered %d of %d (%.2f)' % (
+        ra, na, ra / float(na), rw, nw, rw / float(nw), rl, nl, rl / float(nl)))
+
+
+def entry_text(s):
+    return NL + NL.join(_entry_lines(s)) + NL
+
+
+def _repair_words(r, why):
+    if not r:
+        return 'CARRIED: %s.' % why
+    return ('TAKEN UP BY (R326)(%s (relay data/b716_ferry.txt :%d); its diff and its two-instance control in relay '
+            'data/b716_tool_repairs.txt.' % (r.replace('(', ')(', 1), FERRY_LINES[r]))
+
+
+def _entry_lines(s):
+    L = [TITLE, '',
+         ('b715’s defects bank (relay data/b715_defects.txt) carries seventeen letters (a)-(q), and its events bank (relay '
+          'data/b715_events.txt) eleven starting lines (:%s), entered after its seal and not lettered, for this act’s corrigendum under '
+          'standing clauses v2 row (52). Each item carries NEW or REPEAT, a REPEAT naming the earlier act and letter of its class and the '
+          'bank line or the row of relay data/b675_corrigenda.txt that records it; the evidence set is that bank’s rows (1)-(%d), the '
+          'defects banks of b691-b714 at the lines named, and b715’s replay findings. The six defects whose heads read a refusal, (%s), are '
+          'marked REFUSED BY THE HOOK: each stands as the seat’s attempt, and the record shows the rule held. Nine events are lettered '
+          'as defects, each with its owner, (r)-(z) after b715’s letters, by relay tools/act_letters.py; two are entered as records. '
+          'Each lettered item names the repair of (R326)(3) that takes it up, or reads CARRIED with its reason. b715’s events and defects '
+          'banks, its face, its trail record (PLACE-papers OPEN_TRAILS :%d) and every root-named bank are not edited.' % (
+              ', :'.join(str(n) for n in s['starts']), s['corrigenda_last'], ', '.join(s['refused']), K.B711_TRAIL)), '',
+         '**b715’s defects:**', '']
+    for (x, _h, own, r, words, col, why) in DEFECTS:
+        mark = ' REFUSED BY THE HOOK.' if x in s['refused'] else ''
+        L += ['**(%s) %s** (relay data/b715_defects.txt; %s; %s).%s %s' % (x, words[0].upper() + words[1:], own, _column(col), mark,
+                                                                          _repair_words(r, why)), '']
+    L += ['**b715’s events, lettered:**', '']
+    for (n, _h, own, r, words, col, why), x in zip(LETTERED, s['letters']):
+        L += ['**(%s) %s** (relay data/b715_events.txt :%d; %s; %s). %s' % (x, words[0].upper() + words[1:], n, own, _column(col),
+                                                                            _repair_words(r, why)), '']
+    L += ['**Records, unlettered:** ' + '; '.join(':%d %s (%s)' % (n, w, _column(c)) for n, _h, w, c in RECORDS) +
+          ' (relay data/b715_events.txt), each carried for the author’s word.', '',
+          '**The repeat fraction:** REPEAT %s.' % fraction_words(), '',
+          'The sealed face, the events and defects banks, the trail record and every root-named bank are not edited -- this entry is their '
+          'correction. Nothing here proves RH or GRH.', '',
+          ('*Filed at b716 on the author’s ruling `(R326)`(2): generated by relay `tools/b716_corrigendum.py`, written whole at b716 in the '
+           'corrigendum form of relay `tools/b678_corrigendum.py` (its reader imported unchanged), its letters from relay `tools/act_letters.py`, '
+           'banked as relay `data/b716_corrigendum.txt`, the corrigenda bank `data/b675_corrigenda.txt` appended at row (36); it answers b715’s '
+           'events and defects.*')]
+    return L
+
+
+def bank_lines(s, landed_line=None):
+    ra, na, rw, nw, rl, nl = fraction()
+    L = ['b716 -- THE CORRIGENDUM ENTRY FOR b715`S EVENTS AND DEFECTS, (R326)(2) (%s)' % time.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ', time.gmtime()), '',
+         '### the figures read before the write: the starting lines :%s ; b715`s letters %s ; refused by the hook %s ; the letters after them %s ;' % (
+             ', :'.join(str(n) for n in s['starts']), ''.join(s['prev']), ''.join(s['refused']), ''.join(s['letters'])),
+         '### every REPEAT`s line or row read and its needle found ; the repairs` ferry lines :12-:17 ; the corrigenda bank`s last row (%d).' % (
+             s['corrigenda_last']), '']
+    L += ['  ' + l for l in _entry_lines(s)]
+    L += ['', '### ### **ITEMS %d: b715`S DEFECTS %d (REFUSED BY THE HOOK %d) ; EVENTS LETTERED %d (r)-(z) ; RECORDS %d ; REPAIRS NAMED %d, CARRIED %d ; '
+              'REPEAT %d OF %d WITH THE REFUSED, %d OF %d WITHOUT ; THE FACE, THE EVENTS AND DEFECTS BANKS, THE TRAIL AND THE ROOT-NAMED BANKS '
+              'UNEDITED ; THE ENTRY AT FINDINGS :%s.**' % (
+                  na, len(DEFECTS), len(s['refused']), len(LETTERED), len(RECORDS),
+                  sum(1 for d in DEFECTS if d[3]) + sum(1 for x in LETTERED if x[3]),
+                  sum(1 for d in DEFECTS if not d[3]) + sum(1 for x in LETTERED if not x[3]), ra, na, rw, nw,
+                  landed_line if landed_line else 'NOT YET WRITTEN')]
+    return L
+
+
+def _membrane(p):
+    """standing clauses v2 row (53): the membrane before a write under relay data/ (tools/act_membrane.py guard_path)."""
+    import act_membrane as MEM
+    MEM.guard_path('b716', p, REC.R.ROOT_EXCLUDE)
+
+
+def _put(name, data):
+    p = os.path.join(D, name)
+    _membrane(p)
+    with open(p + '.tmp', 'wb') as fh:
+        fh.write(data.encode('utf-8'))
+    os.replace(p + '.tmp', p)
+
+
+def _row_text(ln):
+    ra, na, rw, nw, rl, nl = fraction()
+    return ROW % ('%d of %d' % (ra, na), '%.2f, without the six refused by the hook %d of %d (%.2f), among the lettered %d of %d' % (
+        ra / float(na), rw, nw, rw / float(nw), rl, nl), ln)
+
+
+def main(route):
+    s = compose()
+    text = entry_text(s)
+    if route == 'dry':
+        REC.R.screens([('FINDINGS.md', TITLE, conv(text))], 'corrigendum', True)
+        print(conv(text))
+        print(NL.join(bank_lines(s)))
+        print(_row_text('NOT YET WRITTEN'))
+        return
+    REC.land([('FINDINGS.md', TITLE, text)], 'b716_corrigendum_landing.json', 'corrigendum', entry=None, dry=False)
+    ln = (REC.jl('b716_corrigendum_landing.json').get('lines') or [{}])[0].get('line')
+    L = bank_lines(s, ln)
+    _put('b716_corrigendum.txt', NL.join(L) + NL)
+    j = dict(at=time.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ', time.gmtime()), entry_line=ln, title=TITLE,
+             defects=[dict(letter=x, owner=o, refused=(x in s['refused']), repair=r, ferry_line=FERRY_LINES.get(r) if r else None,
+                           column=('REPEAT' if c else 'NEW'), repeat_of=(list(c[:4]) if c else None), carried=(why if not r else None))
+                      for (x, _h, o, r, _w, c, why) in DEFECTS],
+             lettered=[dict(letter=x, line=n, owner=o, repair=r, ferry_line=FERRY_LINES.get(r) if r else None,
+                            column=('REPEAT' if c else 'NEW'), repeat_of=(list(c[:4]) if c else None), carried=(why if not r else None))
+                       for (n, _h, o, r, _w, c, why), x in zip(LETTERED, s['letters'])],
+             records=[dict(line=n, column=('REPEAT' if c else 'NEW'), repeat_of=(list(c[:4]) if c else None)) for n, _h, _w, c in RECORDS],
+             starts=s['starts'], refused=s['refused'], row=36, fraction=list(fraction()))
+    _put('b716_corrigendum.json', json.dumps(j, indent=1, ensure_ascii=False) + NL)
+    print(L[-1])
+    print(_row_text(ln))
+
+
+if __name__ == '__main__':
+    main(sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 'dry')

+R._standins = _standins
+READERS = ['read_branch_row', 'read_defect_row', 'read_runner_row', 'read_score_row', 'read_head']
+read_branch_row, read_runner_row = R.read_branch_row, R.read_runner_row
+read_score_row, read_head = R.read_score_row, R.read_head
+
+# ================================================================================ b703'S TO b715'S REPAIRED READERS, THROUGH b715'S MODULE
+_deps = P3._deps                   # ### the primed names read (b703 (f))
+R._deps = _deps
+DEFECT_ROW = R.DEFECT_ROW
+read_defect_row = P3.read_defect_row
+_defect_short = P3._defect_short   # ### the act PASSED AT THE CALL (b700's repair)
+R._defect_short = _defect_short
+_last_export, span_line = P3._last_export, P3.span_line
+_read_verdict, CAP = P4._read_verdict, P4.CAP   # ### b704's repaired verdict (offset, limit VALUES, the cap of two hundred)
+_print_modules = P5._print_modules              # ### b705's print-list reader (every name mapped to its module by git grep bounded to the tree)
+_artifact_named = P6._artifact_named            # ### b706's: the run's artifact chosen by name
+roster709 = P7.roster709                        # ### b707's rowgen roster re-pointed through data/b706_partners.json (the fourth path reader), through b715's module (a carry-time repair: the substitution shifted b715's own name for it)
+
+
+def defshort(*a):
+    act = a[0] if a else ''
+    out = [(s, 'data/%s_defects.txt' % act) for s in _defect_short(act)]
+    for s, src in out:
+        print('  %s   [%s]' % (s, src))
+    print('### ### **%s: DEFECTS %d -- FROM data/%s_defects.txt.**' % (act, len(out), act))
+
+
+# ================================================================================ STEP ZERO: ROWGEN, ANSWERS, DEFECTS (b698's routes, carried; rowgen's roster b715's)
+answers = R.answers
+
+
+def rowgen(*a):
+    """b670 rules row 3: tools/rowgen/rowgen.py `constellation <root> <papers>` and tools/rowgen/diff_rowpin.py `<namemap.json> <papers>` (the
+    build-free diff) over the 19 roster tables, each at the file of record after b706's renames (b715's roster707); each tool's verdict lines
+    banked with its exit. data/b716_rowgen_<stage>.txt."""
+    stage = a[0] if a and a[0] not in ('dry',) else 'stepzero'
+    Ro = roster709()
+    paths = [os.path.join(PP, p) for _n, p, _f in Ro]
+    miss = [p for p in paths if not os.path.exists(p)]
+    if len(Ro) != 19 or miss:
+        sys.exit('### THE ROSTER IS NOT 19 PATHS AT HEAD: %d, missing %s' % (len(Ro), miss))
+    moved = [(n, f, p) for n, p, f in Ro if f]
+    t0 = time.time()
+    con = subprocess.run([sys.executable, '-X', 'utf8', os.path.join(T, 'rowgen', 'rowgen.py'), 'constellation', PP] + paths,
+                         capture_output=True, text=True, encoding='utf-8', errors='replace', timeout=500)
+    dif = subprocess.run([sys.executable, '-X', 'utf8', os.path.join(T, 'rowgen', 'diff_rowpin.py'), os.path.join(T, 'rowgen', 'namemap.json')] + paths,
+                         capture_output=True, text=True, encoding='utf-8', errors='replace', timeout=500)
+    secs = int(time.time() - t0)
+    co, do = (con.stdout or '') + (con.stderr or ''), (dif.stdout or '') + (dif.stderr or '')
+    tail = lambda t: [l for l in t.replace(chr(13), '').split(NL) if l.strip()][-12:]   # noqa: E731
+    L = ['%s -- %s: ROWGEN DIFF AND CONSTELLATION MODE (b670 rules row 3) (%s)' % (ACT, stage.upper(), utc()), '',
+         '### the roster: the 19 tables of relay data/b586_corroboration.txt :34 at PLACE-papers HEAD %s, each at its file of record after b706`s renames '
+         '((R316)(3): %d of the bank`s paths re-pointed from the retired versioned name, data/b706_partners.json, b715`s reader) ; the instruments: tools/rowgen/rowgen.py '
+         '`constellation`, tools/rowgen/diff_rowpin.py (build-free; `rowgen.py diff` runs lean and is not run on this host) ; %d s' % (
+             g(PP, 'rev-parse', '--short', 'HEAD').strip(), len(moved), secs), '']
+    L += ['### re-pointed: %s -> %s' % (f, p) for _n, f, p in moved]
+    L += ['', '### constellation, exit %d ; its last lines:' % con.returncode] + ['    ' + l for l in tail(co)] + [
+         '', '### diff_rowpin, exit %d ; its last lines:' % dif.returncode] + ['    ' + l for l in tail(do)] + [
+         '', '### ### **CONSTELLATION EXIT %d ; ROWGEN DIFF EXIT %d ; TABLES 19 ; RE-POINTED %d.**' % (con.returncode, dif.returncode, len(moved))]
+    put_txt('%s_rowgen_%s.txt' % (ACT, stage), L)
+    print(NL.join(L[-14:]))
+
+
+# ================================================================================ (R326)(2)(i), STANDING CLAUSES v2 ROW (52): THE LETTERS AT THE SEAL
+def letters_of(text):
+    """the lettered rows of a defects bank's text, each '(x) HEAD' by b703's row reader (pure in its argument)."""
+    L = (text or '').replace(chr(13), '').split(NL)
+    return [s for s in (read_defect_row(L, i) for i, l in enumerate(L) if DEFECT_ROW.match(l)) if s]
+
+
+def _letter(s):
+    m = re.match(r"^\(([a-z]'?)\)", s or '')
+    return m.group(1) if m else None
+
+
+def letters_at_seal(act):
+    """the letters entered before the seal: the list the suite's seal records with the hashes (data/<act>_seal_hashes.json `letters`, from b716);
+    for an act sealed before the repair, its defects bank as committed at the seal's relay head (`relay_head`, read by b698's _show). None where
+    the act is not sealed or the bank is not read."""
+    sj = jl('%s_seal_hashes.json' % act)
+    if not sj:
+        return None
+    if isinstance(sj.get('letters'), list):
+        return list(sj['letters'])
+    t = _show(ROOT, sj['relay_head'], 'data/%s_defects.txt' % act) if sj.get('relay_head') else None
+    return letters_of(t) if t is not None else None
+
+
+def defects(*a):
+    """data/b716_defects.json from the bank; after the seal (row (52)) it refuses a letter the seal did not record: a finding after the seal is
+    entered in data/b716_events.txt for the closing's events section, and the next act's corrigendum letters it."""
+    t = rdn('%s_defects.txt' % ACT)
+    ds = [l.strip() for l in t.split(NL) if DEFECT_ROW.match(l)]
+    seal = letters_at_seal(ACT)
+    if seal is not None:
+        late = [s for s in letters_of(t) if _letter(s) not in set(_letter(x) for x in seal)]
+        if late:
+            sys.exit('### (R326)(2)(i): %d LETTER(S) ENTERED AFTER THE SEAL (%s) -- A FINDING AFTER THE SEAL IS AN EVENT, ENTERED IN data/%s_events.txt, '
+                     'NOT A LETTER -- NOTHING WRITTEN' % (len(late), ', '.join(s[:3] for s in late), ACT))
+    put_json('%s_defects.json' % ACT, dict(at=utc(), defects=ds, short=_defect_short(ACT)))
+    print('  defects %d' % len(ds))
+
+
+# ================================================================================ (R326)(2): THE ACT'S STEP BANK (G-SEALED-AFTER-COMPONENTS reads it)
+# ### (R326)(3)(ii): 'gate' -- the commit of the gate before the lock, the one commit the face cannot carry (it stamps the face); the suite's
+# ### G-NOTHING-RAN-AHEAD reads it from this bank and asks every other commit before the lock to be declared on the face by its hash
+PRE_SEAL_PHASES = ('step zero', 'component 1', 'component 2', 'component 3', 'component 4', 'pilot', 'component 5', 'component 6', 'gate')  # pilot: b715's word (2); component 6: b716's pre-lock commit of the face and the act's tools
+POST_SEAL_PHASES = ('seal', 'pre-root', 'root', 'record', 'closing')
+STEP_PHASES = PRE_SEAL_PHASES + POST_SEAL_PHASES
+STEP_REPOS = dict(relay=ROOT, pp=PP)
+
+
+def step(*a):
+    """`step <relay|pp> <phase words> [commit ...]`: one row appended to data/b716_steps.json after a step's commits -- the repository, the
+    commits (HEAD where none is named), the last one's committer time and subject as read, and the phase the seat names from STEP_PHASES;
+    G-SEALED-AFTER-COMPONENTS reads the phase and not the subject. (R326)(4)(ii), standing clauses v4 row (63): EVERY COMMIT OF A STEP -- a
+    step of several commits is one row carrying the list of them (`commits`), `commit` its first, and every arm reads the list through
+    tools/act_close.py step_commits (a repair at b716). A commit already in the bank is not entered twice."""
+    if len(a) < 2 or a[0] not in STEP_REPOS:
+        sys.exit('### step <relay|pp> <phase> [commit ...] -- the phases: %s' % ' | '.join(STEP_PHASES))
+    rest = list(a[1:])
+    commits = []
+    while rest and re.match(r'^[0-9a-f]{7,40}$', rest[-1]):
+        commits.insert(0, rest.pop())
+    commits = commits or ['HEAD']
+    phase = ' '.join(rest)
+    if phase not in STEP_PHASES:
+        sys.exit('### THE PHASE %r IS NOT ONE OF %s -- NOTHING WRITTEN' % (phase, ' | '.join(STEP_PHASES)))
+    fulls = []
+    for commit in commits:
+        full = g(STEP_REPOS[a[0]], 'rev-parse', '--verify', '--quiet', commit + '^{commit}').strip()
+        if not full:
+            sys.exit('### %s IS NOT A COMMIT OF %s -- NOTHING WRITTEN' % (commit, a[0]))
+        fulls.append(full)
+    ct, subj = g(STEP_REPOS[a[0]], 'log', '-1', '--format=%ct%x09%s', fulls[-1]).strip().split('\t', 1)
+    j = jl('%s_steps.json' % ACT)
+    rows = j.get('rows') or []
+    have = set(AC.step_commits(rows, repo=a[0]))
+    dup = [f[:8] for f in fulls if f in have]
+    if dup:
+        sys.exit('### %s %s IN THE STEP BANK ALREADY -- NOTHING WRITTEN' % (a[0], dup))
+    row = dict(repo=a[0], commit=fulls[0], ct=int(ct), at=time.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ', time.gmtime(int(ct))), phase=phase, subject=subj[:120])
+    if len(fulls) > 1:
+        row['commits'] = fulls
+    rows.append(row)
+    put_json('%s_steps.json' % ACT, dict(at=utc(), rows=rows))
+    print('  step %d: %s %s %s -- %s' % (len(rows), a[0], ' '.join(f[:8] for f in fulls), rows[-1]['at'], phase))
+
+
+# ================================================================================ STEP ZERO: THE BRANCHES BANK ((R326)(8); b715's form)
+def branches(*a):
+    """`branches before`: data/b716_branches_before.json -- b698's route, carried (every branch (R326)(8) names, its tip resolved, merged into
+    main by ancestry, the remote's heads read once per repository; written before any delete). `branches`: data/b716_branches.txt (.json) --
+    each branch's fate read now against the tips banked before; the kept branch port-b707 read beside them."""
+    if a and a[0] == 'before':
+        return R.branches('before')
+    before = jl('%s_branches_before.json' % ACT).get('rows') or []
+    if not before:
+        sys.exit('### data/b716_branches_before.json IS NOT WRITTEN -- NOTHING READ')
+    rows = []
+    for r in before:
+        if r['where'] == 'local':
+            gone = not g(r['repo'], 'branch', '--list', r['branch']).strip()
+        else:
+            gone = r['branch'] not in R._remote_heads(r['repo'])
+        rows.append(dict(repo=r['repo'], branch=r['branch'], where=r['where'], tip=r['tip'], merged=r['merged'], fate='DELETED' if gone else 'KEPT'))
+    for r in rows:
+        read_branch_row(r)
+    krepo, kb = K.KEPT_BRANCH
+    kept_local = g(krepo, 'rev-parse', '--verify', '--quiet', 'refs/heads/' + kb).strip()
+    kept_remote = R._remote_heads(krepo).get(kb, '')
+    L = ['%s -- STEP ZERO, (R326)(8): THE BRANCHES, READ BEFORE ANY DELETE AND AFTER (%s)' % (ACT, utc()), '',
+         '### rows are branches: repository | branch | where | the tip before the delete (data/b716_branches_before.json, read before any delete) |',
+         '### merged into main by ancestry | the fate read now. Each delete one branch per command, each its own message, nothing chained after;',
+         '### the listing `git branch --merged main` per repository with no pattern. (R326)(7) names relay`s four and PLACE-papers` two; port-b707 of',
+         '### SIDE-lv-conservation is NOT deleted, local or remote, read beside them.']
+    L += ['  %-22s | %-28s | %-6s | %s | merged %s | %s' % (os.path.basename(r['repo']), r['branch'], r['where'], r['tip'], r['merged'], r['fate'])
+          for r in rows]
+    L += ['  %-22s | %-28s | kept   | local %s ; remote %s ; %s' % (os.path.basename(krepo), kb, kept_local[:12] or 'absent', kept_remote[:12] or 'absent',
+                                                                'STANDS' if kept_local == kept_remote == K.PORT_TIP else '### NOT AS KEPT')]   # ### a carry-time repair: b715's tip, K.PORT_TIP
+    L += ['', '### ### **BRANCHES NAMED %d ; DELETED %d ; KEPT %d ; EVERY TIP MERGED %s ; port-b707 STANDS %s.**' % (
+        len(rows), sum(1 for r in rows if r['fate'] == 'DELETED'), sum(1 for r in rows if r['fate'] == 'KEPT'), all(r['merged'] for r in rows),
+        kept_local == kept_remote == K.PORT_TIP)]
+    put_txt('%s_branches.txt' % ACT, L)
+    put_json('%s_branches.json' % ACT, dict(at=utc(), rows=rows, extra=[], kept=dict(repo=krepo, branch=kb, local=kept_local, remote=kept_remote)))
+    print(NL.join(L[7:]))
+
+
+# ================================================================================ THE READ BANK (b704's repaired route; the word's time the anchor's, from b705)
+def reads(*a):
+    """b704's repaired route (tools/b704_record.py :392) with the author's word at b704's arm-baseline prompt applied: from b705 the word's time
+    is the act's anchor and every call counts -- every Read call of the seat since the anchor with its offset and limit VALUES, the file's line
+    count read now beside it; a call of a file of two hundred lines or more with no offset, or no limit, or a limit above two hundred is an
+    offence. data/b716_read_calls.txt."""
+    word = _anchor_ts(K.ANCHOR)
+    rows = []
+    for i, o, _st, _sid in R._session_rows():
+        if o.get('type') != 'assistant':
+            continue
+        for c in (o.get('message') or {}).get('content') or []:
+            if isinstance(c, dict) and c.get('type') == 'tool_use' and c.get('name') == 'Read':
+                inp = c.get('input') or {}
+                p = inp.get('file_path') or ''
+                rows.append(dict(line=i, ts=o.get('timestamp'), path=p, offset=inp.get('offset'), limit=inp.get('limit'), lines=R._lines_now(p)))
+    for r in rows:
+        r['verdict'] = _read_verdict(r, word)
+    bad = [r for r in rows if r['verdict'] == '### AN OFFENCE']
+    pre = [r for r in rows if r['verdict'].startswith('BEFORE THE WORD')]
+    L = ['%s -- THE READ CALLS SINCE THE ANCHOR, BY data/b698_rules.txt ROW (2) AND THE AUTHOR`S WORD AT b704`S SORT (%s)' % (ACT, utc()), '',
+         '### the rule: whole where the file is under two hundred lines; otherwise an offset and a limit, the limit no greater than two hundred (the',
+         '### cap, the author`s word at %s). Rows are Read calls: transcript line | time | the file`s lines read now | offset | limit | the verdict |' % (word or '### NOT READ'),
+         '### path. From b705 the word`s time is the act`s anchor and every call counts (the author`s word at b704`s arm-baseline prompt).']
+    L += ['  %s | %s | %s | offset %s | limit %s | %s | %s' % (r['line'], r['ts'], r['lines'], r['offset'], r['limit'], r['verdict'], r['path']) for r in rows] or ['  NONE']
+    L += ['', '### ### **READ CALLS %d ; ON FILES OF 200 LINES OR MORE %d ; OFFENCES %d ; THE CAP`S PRECURSORS %d ; THE WORD AT %s.**' % (
+        len(rows), sum(1 for r in rows if r['lines'] is None or r['lines'] >= CAP), len(bad), len(pre), word or '### NOT READ')]
+    if 'dry' not in a:
+        put_txt('%s_read_calls.txt' % ACT, L)
+        put_json('%s_read_calls.json' % ACT, dict(at=utc(), word=word, cap=CAP, rows=rows))
+    print(NL.join([l for l in L if '### AN OFFENCE' in l or 'PRECURSOR' in l][:40] + L[-1:]))
+
+
+def names(*a):
+    """the functions of an imported record module (b698's by default) with the line each opens at -- names and numbers, no source printed."""
+    import importlib
+    import inspect
+    mod = {'b698': R, 'b703': P3, 'b704': P4, 'b705': P5, 'b706': P6, 'b715': P7}.get(a[0] if a else 'b698') or importlib.import_module(a[0])
+    for n, f in sorted(vars(mod).items(), key=lambda kv: inspect.getsourcelines(kv[1])[1] if inspect.isfunction(kv[1]) and kv[1].__module__ == mod.__name__ else 0):
+        if inspect.isfunction(f) and f.__module__ == mod.__name__:
+            print('  %-28s :%d' % (n, inspect.getsourcelines(f)[1]))
+
+
+def verdicts(*a):
+    """prints each named bank's verdict line (its last `### ### **...**` line), for the suite's predicates to quote; writes nothing."""
+    for n_ in a:
+        m = re.findall(r'(?ms)^\s*### ### \*\*(.*?)\*\*\s*$', rdn(n_))
+        print('  %s | %s' % (n_, ' '.join(m[-1].split()) if m else '### NO VERDICT LINE'))
+
+
+# ================================================================================ THE DISPATCH, THE RUNNER (b698's routes; b705's print-list check; b706's artifact by name)
+runstatus, runner = R.runstatus, R.runner
+STD3 = P3.STD3
+EF = K.EF_DIR
+WF = getattr(K, 'WORKFLOW', '')   # ### a carry-time repair: the worklist names no workflow constant; the dispatches name build-module.yml in the route
+
+
+def dispatch(*a):
+    """`dispatch <kernel> <ref sha> <label> <target> [dry]`: b698's dispatch (one dispatch per tree state, the run id read), with b705's check
+    (data/b705_rules.txt row (1)): every name of print_targets.txt at the ref mapped to its module by git grep bounded to the kernel's tree; a
+    target not covering every module is REFUSED, nothing dispatched; the row banked carries `whole` True with the modules the list needs."""
+    kernel, ref, label, target = a[0], a[1], a[2], a[3]
+    mods = _print_modules708('D:/' + kernel, ref)
+    need = sorted(set(m for m in mods.values() if m))
+    unmapped = [n for n, m in mods.items() if not m]
+    tgt = target.split()
+    whole = bool(need) and not unmapped and all(m in tgt for m in need)
+    print('  the print list %d names ; their modules %d %s ; unmapped %s ; the target %d modules ; whole %s' % (len(mods), len(need), need[:6], unmapped or 'NONE', len(tgt), whole))
+    if not whole:
+        sys.exit('### THE TARGET DOES NOT COVER THE PRINT LIST`S MODULES (data/b705_rules.txt row (1)) -- NOT DISPATCHED')
+    R.dispatch(*a)
+    if 'dry' in a[4:]:
+        return
+    j = jl('%s_dispatches.json' % ACT)
+    rows = j.get('rows') or []
+    if rows:
+        rows[-1].update(whole=True, print_names=len(mods), print_modules=need)
+        put_json('%s_dispatches.json' % ACT, dict(rows=rows))
+    print('  the row banked with whole True ; print modules %d' % len(need))
+
+
+def runwait(*a):
+    """`runwait <kernel> <run id> [seconds]`: the run's status read every 30 s in the FOREGROUND until it completes or the seconds pass (at most
+    560, under the 590 s guard); each read printed. No wait is backgrounded."""
+    end = time.time() + min(int(a[2]) if len(a) > 2 else 540, 560)
+    while True:
+        rc, out, err = R._gh('run', 'view', a[1], '-R', 'psinary-sketch/' + a[0], '--json', 'status,conclusion,updatedAt')
+        j = json.loads(out or b'{}')
+        print('  %s  status %s ; conclusion %s ; updated %s' % (utc(), j.get('status'), j.get('conclusion') or '-', j.get('updatedAt')), flush=True)
+        if j.get('status') == 'completed' or time.time() + 30 > end:
+            return
+        time.sleep(30)
+
+
+# ================================================================================ THE LATE ROUTES (b715's, carried with b716's names; the previous window in its own session)
+import edit_route as ER            # noqa: E402
+
+
+def capture():
+    """b701's capture: the seat's session from K.ANCHOR, then each continuation (K.SESSIONS_AFTER) from its own anchor."""
+    rows = ER.capture(SESSION, K.ANCHOR, SUBAGENTS)
+    for sid, anc in K.SESSIONS_AFTER:
+        more = ER.capture('%s/%s.jsonl' % (PROJ, sid), anc, '%s/%s/subagents' % (PROJ, sid))
+        if not more:
+            sys.exit('### THE CONTINUATION SESSION %s READ NO COMMAND (the anchor %r not found as a user line) -- NOTHING WRITTEN' % (sid, anc))
+        rows += [dict(r, src=r['src'] + ' ' + sid[:8]) for r in more]
+    rows.sort(key=lambda r: r['ts'])
+    bg = R._bg_calls()
+    for i, r in enumerate(rows):
+        r['n'] = i + 1
+        r['bg'] = (r.get('ts'), r.get('cmd')) in bg
+    return rows
+
+
+R.capture = capture
+_iso_epoch, _git_ct, _files_of, _ledger_adds, _amended_after = R._iso_epoch, R._git_ct, R._files_of, R._ledger_adds, R._amended_after
+
+
+def prev_capture():
+    """G-EDIT-ROUTE's window over the previous act, b701's form for a previous act in ITS OWN session (b715's carry-time repair, carried): b715's
+    capture AFTER ITS POST-PUSH SUITE -- every command of session 0be08567 from the call that combined b715's post-push parts into
+    data/b715_checks_postpush.txt to that session's end, its closing's commands among them; this act runs in a session of its own."""
+    rows = ER.capture(PREV_SESSION_PATH, K.PREV_ANCHOR, PREV_SUBAGENTS)
+    first = [r['ts'] for r in rows if '%s_checks_postpush' % PREV in r['cmd'] and '--combine' in r['cmd']]
+    if not first:
+        sys.exit('### THE PREVIOUS ACT`S POST-PUSH COMBINE IS NOT IN ITS SESSION`S CAPTURE -- NOTHING READ')
+    anchor = '9999'   # ### b716's carry-time repair: the previous act ran in ITS OWN session (b715 bounded it at this act's anchor, b700's same-session form), so its window runs to that session's end (b701's form)
+    out = [dict(r, src='%s %s' % (PREV, r['src'])) for r in rows if first[0] <= r['ts'] < anchor]
+    for i, r in enumerate(out):
+        r['n'] = 'p%d' % (i + 1)
+    return out
+
+
+R.prev_capture = prev_capture
+edit_route_offences = R.edit_route_offences
+QUOTED, REDIRECT_NULL, REDIRECT_ANY, PIPE_FILTER, PY_PRINT_READ, MOVE_OR_DELETE, SED_KIND = (
+    R.QUOTED, R.REDIRECT_NULL, R.REDIRECT_ANY, R.PIPE_FILTER, R.PY_PRINT_READ, R.MOVE_OR_DELETE, R.SED_KIND)
+local_lean, delete_shape, chained_after, seal_hashes, root_banks_state = R.local_lean, R.delete_shape, R.chained_after, R.seal_hashes, R.root_banks_state
+root_banks, rootbanks, root, root_arm, table_final = R.root_banks, R.rootbanks, R.root, R.root_arm, R.table_final
+# ### b715's pattern read `_record.py dispatch`; this act's one dispatch ran through the `portdispatch` route, so the pattern takes the port prefix too
+# ### (a carry-time repair, banked by diff: the command bank's first write read DISPATCHES 0 against the dispatch bank's one row)
+DISPATCH_CMD = re.compile(r'(^|&&\s*|;\s*)gh workflow run\b|_record\.py (?:port)?dispatch\b(?![^\n]*\bdry\b)', re.M)
