+def repairctl12(*a):
+    """data/b716_repair_controls.txt (.json), Component 1, (R326)(3): each of the five repairs and the NEXT line's repair run on b715's banks,
+    each with the control the ruling names and a NEG case; every reading printed beside its case. The repaired forms are read where they
+    stand: tools/b716_checks.py (post_seal_named, answers_bank_ok, nothing_ahead_ok), tools/b716_record.py (post_seal_row, mirror_order),
+    tools/b716_closing.py (head_line, state_file_sentence), tools/b716_regspec.py (refresh_commands), tools/b716_worklist.py (orders_naming)."""
+    import shutil
+    import b716_checks as CK
+    import b716_closing as CL
+    import b715_checks as CK7
+    rows, notes = [], []
+
+    def case(sec, name, got, want, note=''):
+        rows.append(dict(section=sec, case=name, got=got, wanted=want, as_wanted=got == want, note=note))
+
+    lock = CK.iso_epoch(jl('b715_seal_hashes.json')['lock'])
+    face7 = rdn('b715_registration_2026-10-25.txt')
+    a7, aj7 = rdn('b715_author_answers.txt'), jl('b715_author_answers.json')
+    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- (i) THE FACE NAMES THE ROOT'S PROMPT BY FORM
+    row1 = post_seal_row(True)
+    face_1 = face7.replace('### POST-SEAL PROMPTS NAMED: NONE', row1)
+    case('i', 'the builder`s row where the order puts the root`s prompt after the lock', row1, '### POST-SEAL PROMPTS NAMED: 1 (the root`s prompt)')
+    case('i', 'the builder`s row where no prompt follows the lock (NEG of the form)', post_seal_row(False), '### POST-SEAL PROMPTS NAMED: NONE')
+    case('i', 'b715`s answers bank under the repaired arm, its face naming 1: passes', CK.answers_bank_ok(a7, aj7, lock, face_1, 'b715', CK7.FLOORS), True)
+    case('i', 'b715`s answers bank under the repaired arm, a face naming 0 (b715`s own, NONE): fails', CK.answers_bank_ok(a7, aj7, lock, face7, 'b715', CK7.FLOORS), False)
+    case('i', 'the arm reads the row`s figure from the form "1 (the root`s prompt)"', CK.post_seal_named(face_1), 1)
+    after = [c.get('ts') for c in aj7.get('calls') or [] if CK.iso_epoch((c.get('ts') or '')[:19] + 'Z') and CK.iso_epoch((c.get('ts') or '')[:19] + 'Z') > lock]
+    notes.append('(i) b715`s lock %s ; its answers bank`s calls after the lock %s ; b715`s face row %r' % (
+        jl('b715_seal_hashes.json')['lock'], after, (re.search(r'### POST-SEAL PROMPTS NAMED: \S+', face7) or [None])[0]))
+    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- (ii) THE GATE'S COMMIT READ FROM THE STEP BANK
+    logs = _b715_logs()
+    steps7 = jl('b715_steps.json').get('rows') or []
+    marked = [dict(r, phase='gate') if r['commit'].startswith('85c26c1c') else r for r in steps7]
+    case('ii', 'b715`s banks pass with 85c26c1c read as the gate (its step row marked gate in a copy)', CK.nothing_ahead_ok(face7, lock, logs, marked), True)
+    case('ii', 'an undeclared commit before the lock fails (NEG)', CK.nothing_ahead_ok(face7, lock, logs + [('deadbeefcafe', 'x', lock - 60)], marked), False)
+    case('ii', 'b715`s step bank as banked, no row marked gate: fails (the gate`s commit undeclared)', CK.nothing_ahead_ok(face7, lock, logs, steps7), False)
+    case('ii', 'b715`s old arm on the same banks (every commit before the lock on the face): fails on 85c26c1c',
+         all(h[:7] in face7 for h, s, t in logs if t <= lock), False)
+    notes.append('(ii) b715`s commits at or before the lock %d ; on the face %d ; not on the face %s' % (
+        len([1 for h, s, t in logs if t <= lock]), len([1 for h, s, t in logs if t <= lock and h[:7] in face7]),
+        [h[:8] for h, s, t in logs if t <= lock and h[:7] not in face7]))
+    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- (iii) THE HEAD LINE BUILT FROM BANKS
+    sp = os.path.join(K.SP, 'b716_ctl_headline_%d' % int(time.time()))   # ### a new scratch directory per run; nothing is deleted
+    os.makedirs(sp)
+    for f in os.listdir(D):
+        if f.startswith('b715_') and (f.endswith('push_out.txt') or f in ('b715_checks_postpush.txt', 'b715_act_root.json', 'b715_author_answers.txt',
+                                                                          'b715_defects.txt', 'b715_scores_close.json')):
+            shutil.copyfile(os.path.join(D, f), os.path.join(sp, f))
+    ev = []
+    hl = CL.head_line(dd=sp, act='b715', events=ev)
+    case('iii', 'b715`s banks rebuild the head line with "1 prompt answered"', '; 1 prompt answered;' in hl, True)
+    case('iii', 'b715`s banks rebuild the head line with "scores 8 of 8 HELD"', '; scores 8 of 8 HELD;' in hl, True)
+    sc = jl('b715_scores_close.json')
+    sc['N2'] = ['BROKEN', 'a planted BROKEN for the control']
+    with open(os.path.join(sp, 'b715_scores_close.json'), 'w', encoding='utf-8') as fh:
+        json.dump(sc, fh, ensure_ascii=False)
+    hl7 = CL.head_line(dd=sp, act='b715', events=[])
+    case('iii', 'a scores bank with one BROKEN prints 7 of 8 (NEG)', '; scores 7 of 8 HELD;' in hl7, True)
+    case('iii', 'b715`s own head line (data/b715_closing.txt :1) carried 2 prompts and no score', ('2 prompts answered' in rdn('b715_closing.txt').split(NL)[0])
+         and 'scores' not in rdn('b715_closing.txt').split(NL)[0], True)
+    notes.append('(iii) rebuilt: %s' % hl)
+    notes.append('(iii) with one BROKEN: %s' % hl7)
+    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- (iv) THE MIRROR'S ORDER
+    import zipfile
+    zl = zipfile.ZipFile('D:/MY-DOwnloads/mirror-refresh-2026-10-25-b715.zip').read('MANIFEST.md').decode('utf-8-sig').replace(chr(13), '').split(NL)
+    root7 = [l for l in zl if l.startswith('Act root: ')]
+    case('iv', 'b715`s sequence replayed on a copy: the bank before mroot (the export`s root line NONE, b715`s root appended) refuses',
+         bool(mirror_order('b715', [])), True)
+    case('iv', 'after mroot (the export`s root line b715`s): the bank may be written', mirror_order('b715', root7), '')
+    case('iv', 'a root line naming another act`s root refuses (NEG)', bool(mirror_order('b715', ['Act root: b710 11fe3e21'])), True)
+    case('iv', 'before the act`s root is appended the order does not refuse (b716 now)', mirror_order('b716', []), '')
+    notes.append('(iv) b715`s export`s root line now %r ; the refusal before mroot: %s' % (root7[:1], mirror_order('b715', [])[:120]))
+    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- (v) THE COMMAND SCREEN READS A FRESH BANK; THE STATE FILE'S BLOB
+    stale = _count_pipes(g(ROOT, 'show', '85c26c1c:data/b715_commands.json'))
+    fresh = _count_pipes(rdn('b715_commands.json'))
+    case('v', 'b715`s stale command bank (at its gate commit 85c26c1c) reproduces ANYPIPES 7', stale, 7)
+    case('v', 'the fresh bank (refreshed after the seal, 13:04Z) reads 8', fresh, 8)
+    blob7, blob11 = g(ROOT, 'rev-parse', 'b5898a61:tools/mirror_prevbuild.json').strip(), g(ROOT, 'rev-parse', '4a383ac8:tools/mirror_prevbuild.json').strip()
+    case('v', 'tools/mirror_prevbuild.json`s blob at b715`s closing (4a383ac8) equal to b707`s (b5898a61): b715`s "committed with this closing" false',
+         bool(blob7) and blob7 == blob11, True)
+    sfs = CL.state_file_sentence()
+    case('v', 'the repaired sentence now: "unchanged since its last commit", not "committed"', 'unchanged since its last commit' in sfs and 'not committed' in sfs, True)
+    notes.append('(v) ANYPIPES stale %s, fresh %s ; the blob at b707 %s, at b715`s closing %s ; the sentence now: %s' % (stale, fresh, blob7[:8], blob11[:8], sfs))
+    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- THE NEXT LINE
+    ot = rd(OT)
+    found = K.orders_naming(ot, 'b716')
+    plant = ot + NL + '*Appended 2026-10-25 by b799 -- THE ORDER **`W-ORD-PLANTED-CONTROL`**: a planted row. Status OPEN, for b716.' + NL
+    case('NEXT', 'a planted open-orders row whose status names b716 is printed by the NEXT line', 'W-ORD-PLANTED-CONTROL' in K.next_line(text=plant), True)
+    case('NEXT', 'the same row is not printed for another act (NEG)', any(o == 'W-ORD-PLANTED-CONTROL' for _i, o, _s in K.orders_naming(plant, 'b716')), False)
+    case('NEXT', 'the NEXT line opens with the ruling`s named items', K.next_line(text=plant).startswith(K.NEXT), True)
+    notes.append('(NEXT) the open-orders rows whose status names b716 on OPEN_TRAILS now: %s' % (found or 'NONE'))
+    notes.append('(NEXT) the line now: %s' % K.next_line()[:400])
+    notes.append('(iii) the scratch copies: %s (left in the scratchpad)' % sp)
+    nw = [r for r in rows if not r['as_wanted']]
+    L = ['%s -- COMPONENT 1, (R326)(3): THE FIVE REPAIRS AND THE NEXT LINE`S, EACH ON b715`S BANKS WITH ITS CONTROL (%s)' % (ACT, utc()), '',
+         '### rows are cases: the section | the case | the reading got | the reading wanted | AS WANTED. The sections: (i) the face names the root`s',

+         '### screen`s fresh bank and the state file`s blob; NEXT the open-orders rows. Each control is (R326)(3)`s own; a NEG case beside it. The',
+         '### head-line copies are made in the scratchpad and removed after the reading.', '']
+    L += ['  %-5s | %-150s | got %-34s | wanted %-34s | %s' % (r['section'], r['case'][:150], str(r['got'])[:34], str(r['wanted'])[:34],
+                                                           'AS WANTED' if r['as_wanted'] else '### NOT AS WANTED -- ' + r['note']) for r in rows]
+    L += ['', '### THE READINGS BESIDE THE CASES:'] + ['  ' + n for n in notes]
+    L += ['', '### ### **CASES %d ; AS WANTED %d ; NOT AS WANTED %d.**' % (len(rows), len(rows) - len(nw), len(nw))]
+    if 'dry' not in a:
+        put_txt('%s_repair_controls.txt' % ACT, L)
+        put_json('%s_repair_controls.json' % ACT, dict(at=utc(), cases=rows, as_wanted=not nw, not_as_wanted=len(nw), notes=notes))
+    print(NL.join(L[7:]))

+REPAIRS712 = (
+    ('(56)', '(R326)(3)(i), THE FACE NAMES THE ROOT`S PROMPT BY FORM',

+REPAIR712_TRACKED = ('tools/b716_record.py', 'tools/b716_worklist.py', 'tools/b716_checks.py', 'tools/b716_closing.py', 'tools/b716_regspec.py')

+def toolrepairs12(*a):
+    """data/b716_tool_repairs.txt (.json), Component 1, (R326)(3): each repair's row (its standing clauses v3 row, its objects, its control's
+    section in data/b716_repair_controls.txt) and the diffs of the five tools against their commit at step zero (relay 46750da9), each whole.
+    The standing report tool's row is appended at Component 5, when its items are written."""
+    ctl = jl('%s_repair_controls.json' % ACT)
+    if not ctl.get('cases'):
+        sys.exit('### data/b716_repair_controls.json IS NOT WRITTEN -- THE CONTROLS FIRST; NOTHING WRITTEN')
+    nw = [c for c in ctl['cases'] if not c.get('as_wanted')]
+    L = ['%s -- COMPONENT 1, (R326)(3): THE FIVE REPAIRS (STANDING CLAUSES v3 ROWS (56)-(60)) AND THE NEXT LINE`S, WITH THEIR DIFFS (%s)' % (ACT, utc()), '',
+         '### rows are repairs: the row | what is repaired | its objects and its control. The controls: relay data/b716_repair_controls.txt (%d cases,' % len(ctl['cases']),
+         '### %d as wanted, %d not as wanted). The diffs follow the rows: each tool against its blob at relay %s (the step-zero commit), `git diff`.' % (
+             len(ctl['cases']) - len(nw), len(nw), K.STEPZERO_BANKS[:8]), '']
+    for n, what, objs in REPAIRS712:
+        L += ['  %s %s.' % (n, what)] + ['      ' + x for x in _wrap_words(objs, 140)] + ['']
+    stat = []
+    for p in REPAIR712_TRACKED:
+        d = g(ROOT, 'diff', K.STEPZERO_BANKS, '--', p).replace(chr(13), '').rstrip(NL)
+        add = sum(1 for l in d.split(NL) if l.startswith('+') and not l.startswith('+++'))
+        rem = sum(1 for l in d.split(NL) if l.startswith('-') and not l.startswith('---'))
+        stat.append((p, add, rem))
+        L += ['', '### %s : +%d -%d' % (p, add, rem)] + (['    ' + l for l in d.split(NL)] if d else ['    (unchanged)'])
+    L += ['', '### ### **REPAIRS %d (FIVE OF v3 ROWS (56)-(60) AND THE NEXT LINE) ; CONTROLS %d, AS WANTED %d, NOT AS WANTED %d ; TOOLS DIFFED %d ; '
+              'NO SEALED TOOL OF AN EARLIER ACT EDITED.**' % (len(REPAIRS712), len(ctl['cases']), len(ctl['cases']) - len(nw), len(nw), len(REPAIR712_TRACKED))]
+    if 'dry' in a:
+        print(NL.join(L[:30] + L[-1:]))
+        return
+    put_txt('%s_tool_repairs.txt' % ACT, L)
+    put_json('%s_tool_repairs.json' % ACT, dict(at=utc(), repairs=[dict(row=n, what=w) for n, w, _o in REPAIRS712], tracked=stat,
+                                                 controls=len(ctl['cases']), not_as_wanted=len(nw)))
+    print(L[-1])
+

+def _full_sentence(l, at):
+    """the whole sentence about a hit (the pilot's _sent bounds, uncut: the pilot's bank prints 240 characters)."""
+    a = max(l.rfind('. ', 0, at), l.rfind('; ', 0, at))
+    a = 0 if a < 0 else a + 2
+    b = min([p for p in (l.find('. ', at), l.find('; ', at)) if p >= 0] or [len(l)])
+    return l[a:b + 1].strip()
+
+
+def rootprompt(*a):
+    """[10], the root's prompt, put once: what b716 inherits from this act, each from its bank -- I. the toolset b716's close runs on ((R326)(3)(v),
+    (12)): its version, the coverage table's reach and the replay's foot and findings; II. the pins census's hard failures, for a clone or a
+    ruling each ((R326)(6)), and upstream Mathlib's revs across the kernels' manifests where the author's word (3)(c) reads one. PRINTS ONLY:
+    the prompt's text is banked by the answers route from the transcript; no bank here."""
+    f = _figs()
+    rp = rdn('%s_replay.txt' % ACT).split(NL)
+    finds = [l.strip() for l in rp if re.match(r'^  F-\d+ \[', l)]
+    pt = rdn('%s_pins_census.txt' % ACT).split(NL)
+    hard = []
+    for i, l in enumerate(pt):
+        if l.strip().startswith('### HARD FAILURE'):
+            head = next((pt[j] for j in range(i, -1, -1) if pt[j].startswith('--- ')), '--- (unnamed)')
+            hard.append('%s -- %s' % (head[4:].split('   ')[0].strip(), l.strip()[4:].strip()))
+    L = ['THE ROOT`S PROMPT (b716, put once) -- I. THE TOOLSET b716 RUNS ON (relay data/b716_toolset_coverage.txt, data/b716_replay.txt):', '',
+         '  tools/standing/ at %s ; %s files ; act literals in the code %s ; the close`s %s steps: %s STANDING (%s), %s PER-ACT, %s standing outside the steps' % (
+             f['ver'], f['sc_files'], f['sc_lit'], f['cv_steps'], f['cv_std'], f['cv_std_at'], f['cv_per'], f['cv_out']),
+         '  the replay over b715 and b713: %s objects, %s EQUAL, %s DIFFERS %s, named %s, unnamed %s ; the findings, the old tools`, not copied forward:' % (
+             f['rp_obj'], f['rp_eq'], f['rp_diff'], f['rp_diffs'], f['rp_named'], f['rp_unnamed'])]
+    L += ['    ' + x for x in finds]
+    L += ['', 'II. THE PINS CENSUS`S %d HARD FAILURES OF %s REPOSITORIES (relay data/b716_pins_census.txt), carried to b716 for a clone or a ruling each:' % (
+        len(hard), f['pins_n'])]
+    L += ['  (%d) %s' % (i, h) for i, h in enumerate(hard, 1)] or ['  none (the census under the author`s word (3): PUBLIC repositories alone, the fork out)']
+    L += ['  upstream Mathlib in the kernels` manifests at %s revs, the repositories carrying each: %s -- the word (3)(c) reads one, de5ce8a9' % (
+        f['pins_ml'], f['pins_ml_list'])]
+    L += ['', '### FINDINGS %d ; HARD FAILURES %d (the foot %s) ; the word is banked in data/b716_author_answers.txt and carried to b716 by the trail' % (
+        len(finds), len(hard), f['pins_hard'])]
+    print(NL.join(L))
+

+# ================================================================================ COMPONENT 5, (R326)(7): THE CHAIN'S CENSUS ROWS

+    (106, 'techne_kernel_voice1.balance_theorem', _SK, 'v1.2', 'Kernel/Voice1.lean', 22, 'decl', ('none', 'no banked print at v1.2')),
+    (109, 'SIDEKernel.formation', _SK, 'v1.2', 'Kernel/Core.lean', 53, 'decl', ('probe', 'b554_probe_k4.txt', 7, 7)),
+    (115, 'SIDELvConservation.PartialPositivity.partialPositivity_finiteRange', _LV, '6efa9e5', 'SIDELvConservation/PartialPositivity.lean', 105, 'decl', ('probe', 'b554_probe_l2.txt', 12, 14)),
+    (118, 'SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.h2_sign', _EF, '38e2734', 'SIDEExplicitFormula/H2Sign.lean', 29, 'decl', ('cell', 'b704_probe_out_zeta.txt', 51)),
+    (119, 'SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.h2_sign_iff_rh', _EF, '38e2734', 'SIDEExplicitFormula/Seam.lean', 101, 'decl', ('cell', 'b704_probe_out_zeta.txt', 71)),
+    (121, 'RiemannHypothesis', None, 'de5ce8a9', 'Mathlib/NumberTheory/LSeries/RiemannZeta.lean', 185, 'mathlib', ('cell', 'b704_probe_out_zeta.txt', 7)),
+    (122, 'faces_join', _FJ, '11a472b', 'FacesJoin.lean', 29, 'decl', ('cell', 'b712_join_grade.txt', 16)),
+]
+_NOHB = (120, 99, 100, 103, 104, 105, 107, 108, 110, 111, 112, 113, 114, 116, 117)   # ### (P3)'s NO HOMEBREW ROW lines
+_KW = re.compile(r'^\s*(?:@\[[^\]]*\]\s*)?(?:private\s+|protected\s+|noncomputable\s+)*(theorem|lemma|def|abbrev|structure|class|instance|axiom|opaque)\b')
+
+
+def _chain_read(repo, pin, path, line, kind):
+    """(sha, statement, kind, span end, sorry lines) of the declaration at `path`:`line` at `pin`, from git show; None when unread."""
+    sha = (gitc(repo, 'rev-parse', '--short=7', pin + '^{commit}') or '').strip()
+    src = gitc(repo, 'show', '%s:%s' % (pin, path))
+    if not src:
+        return None
+    ls = src.replace('\r', '').split(NL)
+    i = line - 1
+    if kind == 'field':
+        ind = len(ls[i]) - len(ls[i].lstrip())
+        j = i + 1
+        while j < len(ls) and ls[j].strip() and len(ls[j]) - len(ls[j].lstrip()) > ind:
+            j += 1
+        return sha, NL.join(ls[i:j]), 'field', j, []
+    m = _KW.match(ls[i])
+    k = m.group(1) if m else '?'
+    j = i
+    while j < len(ls) and not re.search(r':=|\bwhere\b', ls[j]):
+        j += 1
+    st = re.split(r':=|\bwhere\b', NL.join(ls[i:j + 1]), 1)[0].rstrip()
+    if k in ('def', 'abbrev'):
+        e = j + 1
+        while e < len(ls) and ls[e].strip() and not _KW.match(ls[e]) and not ls[e].lstrip().startswith('/-'):
+            e += 1
+        st = NL.join(ls[i:e]).rstrip()
+    e = line
+    while e < len(ls) and not _KW.match(ls[e]) and not re.match(r'^\s*(end|namespace|section)\b', ls[e]):
+        e += 1
+    sl = [line + n for n, t in enumerate(ls[line - 1:e]) if re.search(r'(?<![\w`])sorry\b', t.split('--')[0])]
+    return sha, st, k, e, sl
+
+
+def gitc(repo, *a):
+    r = subprocess.run(['git', '-C', repo] + list(a), capture_output=True, encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+    return r.stdout if r.returncode == 0 else None
+
+
+def _chain_axioms(ax, sha):
+    """(text, source) of a row's axioms: a probe bank counts only when its head names the pin's commit and reads no DIFF."""
+    if ax[0] == 'none':
+        return 'axioms not read at this pin', ax[1]
+    t = (rdn(ax[1]) or '').split(NL)
+    if ax[0] == 'probe':
+        head = t[:4]
+        if not head or sha not in head[0] or any('DIFF' in h for h in head[1:3]):
+            return 'axioms not read at this pin', 'relay data/%s :1-:3 -- %s' % (ax[1], 'the head names another commit' if not head or sha not in head[0]
+                                                                                   else 'the pin`s file printed over a checkout whose file or imports DIFF')
+        return ' '.join(' '.join(t[ax[2] - 1:ax[3]]).split()), 'relay data/%s :%d%s, its head naming %s with no DIFF' % (
+            ax[1], ax[2], '-:%d' % ax[3] if ax[3] != ax[2] else '', sha)
+    return ' '.join(t[ax[2] - 1].split()), 'relay data/%s :%d' % (ax[1], ax[2])
+
+
+def chainrows(*a):
+    """(R326)(7)'s evidence for the census v0.8.2's rows: each of the 24 declarations of (P3) read at its pin, its statement, its grade under
+    the shared E0 rule (tools/e0_rule.py grade), the sorry lines of its span and its axioms where a banked print at the pin reaches them.
+    Banked as data/b716_chain_rows.txt; the census rows are written from it by the Edit tool."""
+    import e0_rule as E0
+    L = ['b716 -- COMPONENT 5, (R326)(7): THE CHAIN`S 24 DECLARATIONS READ AT THEIR PINS, GRADED AND THEIR AXIOMS READ (%s)' % utc(), '',
+         '### rows are declarations, in the order (R326)(7) takes them (the pilot`s NO HOMEBREW ROW names first, rh_strip_imp_rh_holds among them, then',
+         '### the rest): the pilot`s line (relay data/b715_pilot.txt), the name, the repository, pin and place, the statement at the pin (git show',
+         '### <pin>:<path>, from its line to its `:=`; a def whole), its kind, the E0 rule`s grade and why (tools/e0_rule.py grade, from the statement',
+         '### alone), the span`s sorry lines and the axioms. Written by tools/b716_record.py chainrows.', '']
+    grades = {}
+    for pl, name, repo, pin, path, line, kind, ax in CHAIN:
+        if kind == 'mathlib':
+            t = (rdn('b704_probe_out_zeta.txt') or '').split(NL)
+            sha, st, k, e, sl = 'de5ce8a', NL.join(t[3:5]), 'def', None, []
+            where = 'Mathlib de5ce8a9 (SIDE-explicit-formula v0.37 = 38e2734`s lake-manifest.json :10) %s :%d -- the elaborated statement of relay data/b704_probe_out_zeta.txt :4-:5 (run 39721668586 at v0.37; no Mathlib clone at the pin)' % (path, line)
+        else:
+            got = _chain_read(repo, pin, path, line, kind)
+            if got is None:
+                L += ['--- (P3) :%d  %s  ### NOT READ AT %s %s:%d' % (pl, name, pin, path, line), '']
+                continue
+            sha, st, k, e, sl = got
+            where = '%s %s = %s : %s :%d' % (os.path.basename(repo), pin, sha, path, line)
+        if k == 'field':
+            g, why = 'NOT GRADED', 'a structure field: the E0 rule grades a declaration`s statement, and a field is no declaration'
+        else:
+            short = name.split('.')[-1]
+            mm = re.search(r'\b' + re.escape(short) + r'\b', st)
+            try:
+                g, why, _b = E0.grade(st[mm.end():] if mm else st, 'def' if k in ('def', 'abbrev') else 'theorem')
+            except Exception as ex:   # noqa: BLE001
+                g, why = 'UNCLASSED', ' '.join(str(ex).split())[:300]
+        grades[g] = grades.get(g, 0) + 1
+        axt, axs = _chain_axioms(ax, sha if kind != 'mathlib' else '')
+        L += ['--- (P3) :%d  %s%s' % (pl, name, '  (NO HOMEBREW ROW)' if pl in _NOHB else ''), '    at   : ' + where]
+        L += ['    stmt : ' + s for s in st.split(NL)]
+        L += ['    kind %s ; GRADE %s ; %s' % (k, g, why), '    sorry in the span%s : %s' % (' :%d-:%d' % (line, e) if e else '', sl or 'NONE'),
+              '    axioms : %s ; %s' % (axt, axs), '']
+    L.append('### ### **DECLARATIONS %d ; GRADES %s ; AXIOMS READ AT THE PIN %d ; NOT READ AT THIS PIN %d ; SORRY IN A SPAN %d.**' % (
+        len(CHAIN), ', '.join('%s %d' % kv for kv in sorted(grades.items())),
+        sum(1 for l in L if l.startswith('    axioms : ') and 'not read at this pin' not in l),
+        sum(1 for l in L if l.startswith('    axioms : axioms not read at this pin')),
+        sum(1 for l in L if l.startswith('    sorry in the span') and not l.endswith('NONE'))))
+    put_txt('%s_chain_rows.txt' % ACT, L)
+    print('bank data/%s_chain_rows.txt' % ACT)
+    print(L[-1])
+
+
+_C81 = 'phase2/method/THE_KEYSTONE_CENSUS_v0_8_1.md'
+_C82 = 'phase2/method/THE_KEYSTONE_CENSUS_v0_8_2.md'
+
+
+def edition82(*a):
+    """(R326)(7): the census's next edition, v0.8.2, begun as v0.8.1's bytes at PLACE-papers main (git show main:<v0.8.1>), written once beside
+    it; v0.8.1 unedited. Refuses when v0.8.2 exists. The rows and the version lines are then written by the Edit tool."""
+    dst = os.path.join(PP, *_C82.split('/'))
+    if os.path.exists(dst):
+        sys.exit('### %s EXISTS -- NOT WRITTEN' % _C82)
+    r = subprocess.run(['git', '-C', PP, 'show', 'main:' + _C81], capture_output=True)
+    if r.returncode != 0 or not r.stdout:
+        sys.exit('### main:%s NOT READ -- NOT WRITTEN' % _C81)
+    with open(dst, 'xb') as f:
+        f.write(r.stdout)
+    print('wrote %s : %d bytes, sha256 %s ; main:%s sha256 %s' % (_C82, len(r.stdout), hashlib.sha256(open(dst, 'rb').read()).hexdigest(),
+                                                               _C81, hashlib.sha256(r.stdout).hexdigest()))
+
+
+def censusdiff(*a):
+    """(R326)(7)'s diff banked: v0.8.1 at main against v0.8.2's working copy, unified, as data/b716_census_diff.txt."""
+    old = subprocess.run(['git', '-C', PP, 'show', 'main:' + _C81], capture_output=True).stdout.decode('utf-8').replace('\r', '').split(NL)
+    new = open(os.path.join(PP, *_C82.split('/')), encoding='utf-8').read().replace('\r', '').split(NL)
+    import difflib
+    d = list(difflib.unified_diff(old, new, 'main:' + _C81, _C82 + ' (working copy)', lineterm='', n=1))
+    L = ['b716 -- COMPONENT 5, (R326)(7): THE CENSUS v0.8.2 AGAINST v0.8.1, UNIFIED (%s)' % utc(), '',
+         '### v0.8.1 read from PLACE-papers main, v0.8.2 its working copy; v0.8.2 begun as v0.8.1`s bytes (tools/b716_record.py edition82), its',
+         '### additions written by the Edit tool. Written by tools/b716_record.py censusdiff.', ''] + d + [
+        '', '### ### **LINES %d -> %d ; HUNKS %d ; ADDED %d ; REMOVED %d.**' % (len(old), len(new), sum(1 for l in d if l.startswith('@@')),
+                                                                         sum(1 for l in d if l.startswith('+') and not l.startswith('+++')),
+                                                                         sum(1 for l in d if l.startswith('-') and not l.startswith('---')))]
+    put_txt('%s_census_diff.txt' % ACT, L)
+    print('bank data/%s_census_diff.txt' % ACT)
+    print(L[-1])
+
+
+def censusscreen(*a):
+    """(R326)(7): b698's landing screens (the scanner, no-disclosure, backtick parity, unread figures, outside names, stems) over the lines
+    v0.8.2 adds to v0.8.1, dry: the scan file goes to the session scratchpad, nothing to data/. PRINTS ONLY."""
+    old = subprocess.run(['git', '-C', PP, 'show', 'main:' + _C81], capture_output=True).stdout.decode('utf-8').replace('\r', '').split(NL)
+    new = open(os.path.join(PP, *_C82.split('/')), encoding='utf-8').read().replace('\r', '').split(NL)
+    import difflib
+    add = [l[1:] for l in difflib.unified_diff(old, new, lineterm='', n=0) if l.startswith('+') and not l.startswith('+++')]
+    print('### the screens over the %d lines v0.8.2 adds:' % len(add))
+    cells, fired = R.screens([(_C82, 'v0.8.2', NL.join(add) + NL)], 'census82', True)
+    print('### table cells %d (the rows table, expected) ; FIRED %s' % (len(cells), fired))
+
+
+# ### stage one (step zero): b715's Component routes over its own objects pruned (data/b716_prune_record.txt); this act's are added by the Edit tool
+ROUTES = dict(chainrows=chainrows, edition82=edition82, censusdiff=censusdiff, censusscreen=censusscreen,rootprompt=rootprompt,verdicts=verdicts, commands=commands, kernels=kernels, standing=standing, armsecs=armsecs, desk=desk, gatebanks=gatebanks,
+              ksyms=ksyms, defs=defs, figures=figures, runner=runner,
+              mbuild=mbuild, mroot=mroot, mverify=mverify, rootbanks=rootbanks, root=root, root_arm=root_arm, table_final=table_final,
+              names=names, defshort=defshort, rowgen=rowgen, answers=answers, defects=defects, branches=branches, dispatch=dispatch,
+              runstatus=runstatus, runwait=runwait, reads=reads, rootexclude=rootexclude, rootdry=rootdry, linecheck=linecheck,
+              step=step, c1=c1, repairctl=repairctl12, toolrepairs12=toolrepairs12, facerows=facerows, portedit=portedit, portdispatch=portdispatch, portrun=portrun, regpos=regpos, joingrade=joingrade, jointag=jointag,
+              scores=scores, findings=findings, trail=trail, drycheck=drycheck, components=components, mbank=mbank, guard=guard,
+              scorercheck=scorercheck, armnames=armnames, facesdiff=facesdiff, recutadd=recutadd, closeorder=closeorder,
+              repaircontrols=repaircontrols, toolrepairs=toolrepairs13)
+
+if __name__ == '__main__':
+    fn = ROUTES.get(sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else '')
+    if fn is None:
+        print('usage: b716_record.py <%s> [args]' % '|'.join(sorted(ROUTES)))
+        sys.exit(2)
+    fn(*sys.argv[2:])

+    ('events', 'the events bank (an event found after it appended by the Edit tool before the closing)', 'data/<act>_events.txt'),
+    ('scores close', 'scores close', 'data/<act>_scores_close.json'),
+    ('mirror', 'the mirror after the root and the last PLACE-papers push, CLEAN with the root line', 'data/<act>_mirror.txt'),
+    ('report scores', 'the standing report`s scores line, printed after the root and written nowhere', 'the commands bank'),
+    ('closing', 'the closing and its push, captured', 'data/<act>_closing.txt'),
+)
+
+
+def utc():
+    return time.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ', time.gmtime())
+
+
+def cut(fline, opening, heads, numerals):
+    """[(numeral, text)] -- each head to the next head, the last to the line's end, verbatim from the banked ferry's line <fline>."""
+    line = S1.rd(FERRY).split(NL)[fline - 1]
+    if not line.startswith(opening):
+        sys.exit('### THE BANKED FERRY`S :%d DOES NOT OPEN %r -- NOTHING WRITTEN' % (fline, opening))
+    idx = [line.find(h) for h in heads] + [len(line)]
+    if min(idx) < 0 or idx != sorted(idx):
+        sys.exit('### A HEAD NOT FOUND IN ORDER ON :%d (%s) -- NOTHING WRITTEN' % (fline, idx))
+    return [(numerals[k], line[idx[k]:idx[k + 1]].strip()) for k in range(len(heads))]
+
+
+def cuts():
+    """[(fline, title, tag, [(numeral, text)])] for both cuts."""
+    return [(fl, title, tag, cut(fl, op, heads, nums)) for fl, op, heads, title, tag, nums in CUTS]
+
+
+def order_text():
+    return ('THE CLOSE IN ONE ORDER AT v5, THE STEPS NUMBERED (the seat`s row under (R326)(6)(iii), for the author`s strike; v4`s row (68) with the '
+            'root route`s dry run READY True and the combined pre-run bank named as the evidence the seal requires): a step is done when its '
+            'evidence stands, <act> the act`s name, and each closing tool reads its own step here and refuses while a lower step`s evidence is '
+            'absent: ' + '; '.join('[%d] %s (%s)' % (k + 1, t, ev) for k, (_n, t, ev) in enumerate(ORDER)) + '.')
+
+
+def v1_body():
+    """v4's lines from its first LINE section to its last row (the head and the verdict line excluded), byte for byte."""
+    L = S1.rd(V1).split(NL)
+    a = next(i for i, l in enumerate(L) if l.startswith('## LINE '))
+    b = max(i for i, l in enumerate(L) if l.startswith('      '))
+    return L[a:b + 1]
+
+
+def text_of():
+    cs = cuts()
+    body = v1_body()
+    rows1 = S1.parse(S1.rd(V1))
+    n0 = rows1[-1][0]
+    n2 = len(cs[0][3])
+    n6 = len(cs[1][3])
+    L = ['b716 -- THE STANDING CLAUSES, VERSION 5, WRITTEN ONCE AT STEP ZERO UNDER (R326)(2) AND (6): VERSION 4 UNCHANGED (relay data/standing_clauses_v4.txt, its rows '
+         '(1)-(%d) copied byte for byte) WITH (R326)(2)`S FOUR ROWS AS ROWS (%d)-(%d), (R326)(6)`S GOVERNING ROW AND FIVE DELTAS AS ROWS (%d)-(%d) AND THE CLOSE`S '
+         'ORDER AT v5 AS ROW (%d) (%s)' % (n0, n0 + 1, n0 + n2, n0 + n2 + 1, n0 + n2 + n6, n0 + n2 + n6 + 1, utc()), '',
+         'VERSION: 5',
+         'THE CITATION FORM: a ferry names "standing clauses v5" and carries its deltas alone, each delta a numbered row of the next version (data/b710_rules.txt row (1)).',
+         '### rows are clauses, in v1`s form: (n) KIND SEP TAG on the row`s first line, the clause verbatim on the next line after six spaces (v1`s head',
+         '### lines :5-:9 give KIND, SEP and TAG). Rows (1)-(%d) are v4`s, unchanged. Rows (%d)-(%d) are (R326)(2)`s three tiers and visibility map, cut' % (n0, n0 + 1, n0 + n2),
+         '### from relay data/b716_ferry.txt :9; row (%d) governs the deltas and rows (%d)-(%d) are (R326)(6)`s five deltas, cut from :17, each delta' % (
+             n0 + n2 + 1, n0 + n2 + 2, n0 + n2 + n6),
+         '### carrying its control in its own clause ("control: ..."); row (%d) is the close`s order at v5, v4`s row (68) with delta (iii)`s two steps' % (n0 + n2 + n6 + 1),
+         '### given their evidence as the seal`s, the seat`s composition, for the author`s strike. The repairs` diffs and printed controls are the',
+         '### repairs`, not rows. Where a row and an earlier row speak to one object the later row is the version`s reading; no earlier row is edited.', '']
+    L += body
+    n = n0
+    for fl, title, tag, dl in cs:
+        L += ['', '## LINE %d OF data/b716_ferry.txt -- %s' % (fl, title)]
+        for num, t in dl:
+            n += 1
+            tg = '%s, the row that governs the deltas' % tag if num == 'governing' else ('%s(%s)' % (tag, num) if num in ('i', 'ii', 'iii', 'iv', 'v') else '%s, %s' % (tag, num))
+            L.append('  (%d) STANDING STOP %s ; first carried in data/b716_ferry.txt under (R326)' % (n, tg))
+            L.append('      ' + t)
+    L += ['', '## THE CLOSE`S ORDER AT v5 -- (R326)(6)(iii): the steps as one numbered row (the seat`s row, for the author`s strike)']
+    L.append('  (%d) STANDING END SOURCE (R326)(6)(iii), the seat`s row ; written at b716 under (R326)' % (n + 1))
+    L.append('      ' + order_text())
+    L += ['', '### ### **ROWS %d ; v4`S ROWS %d UNCHANGED ; THE THREE TIERS AND THE VISIBILITY MAP ; THE GOVERNING ROW AND FIVE DELTAS (THE AUTHOR`S WORD AFTER '
+          'THE SEAL, THE ACT`S FIGURES FROM ONE SOURCE, EVERY STEP OF THE CLOSE IN THE ORDER, THE STANDING ROWS ENFORCED BY THE HARNESS, ONE LETTERING), EACH '
+          'DELTA WITH ITS CONTROL ; THE CLOSE`S ORDER AT v5, %d STEPS ; VERSION 5.**' % (n + 1, n0, len(ORDER))]
+    return L
+
+
+def _membrane(p):
+    """standing clauses v2 row (53): the membrane before a write under relay data/ (tools/act_membrane.py guard_path)."""
+    import act_membrane as MEM
+    MEM.guard_path('b716', p, None)
+
+
+def write(*a):
+    L = text_of()
+    if 'dry' in a:
+        print(NL.join(L[:12] + L[-34:]))
+        return
+    p = os.path.join(D, BANK)
+    import subprocess
+    tracked = subprocess.run(['git', '-C', ROOT, 'ls-files', '--error-unmatch', 'data/' + BANK], capture_output=True).returncode == 0
+    if tracked or os.path.exists(p):
+        sys.exit('### data/%s EXISTS%s -- A VERSION IS WRITTEN ONCE; NOTHING WRITTEN' % (BANK, ' AND IS COMMITTED' if tracked else ''))
+    _membrane(p)
+    with open(p + '.tmp', 'wb') as fh:
+        fh.write((NL.join(L) + NL).encode('utf-8'))
+    os.replace(p + '.tmp', p)
+    print(L[-1])
+
+
+def control(*a):
+    v1, v2 = S1.rd(V1), S1.rd(BANK)
+    r1, r2 = S1.parse(v1), S1.parse(v2)
+    cs = cuts()
+    flat = [(fl, num, t) for fl, _ti, _tg, dl in cs for num, t in dl]
+    n0 = len(r1)
+    first_equal = r2[:n0] == r1
+    v1_lines_equal = all(l in v2.split(NL) for l in v1_body())
+    dres = []
+    for k, (fl, num, t) in enumerate(flat):
+        row = r2[n0 + k] if len(r2) > n0 + k else None
+        is_delta = fl == 17 and num != 'governing'
+        dres.append(dict(n=n0 + 1 + k, line=fl, row=num, equal=bool(row) and row[4] == t, control_in=(not is_delta) or (bool(row) and 'control:' in row[4]), chars=len(t)))
+    orow = r2[n0 + len(flat)] if len(r2) > n0 + len(flat) else None
+    order_equal = bool(orow) and orow[4] == order_text()
+    neg = v2.replace(NEG_FROM, NEG_TO, 1)
+    neg_rows = S1.parse(neg)
+    k_ii = next(k for k, (fl, num, _t) in enumerate(flat) if fl == 17 and num == 'ii')
+    neg_equal = len(neg_rows) > n0 + k_ii and neg_rows[n0 + k_ii][4] == flat[k_ii][2]
+    ver = re.search(r'^VERSION: (\d+)$', v2, re.M)
+    ok = (first_equal and v1_lines_equal and all(d['equal'] and d['control_in'] for d in dres) and order_equal and len(r2) == n0 + len(flat) + 1
+          and NEG_FROM in v2 and not neg_equal and bool(ver) and ver.group(1) == str(VERSION))
+    L = ['b716 -- STEP ZERO, (R326)(2) AND (6): THE CONTROL OF THE STANDING CLAUSES v5 -- v4`S ROWS UNCHANGED, THE TEN CUT ROWS EQUAL TO THE RULING`S WORDS AND THE ORDER ROW (%s)' % utc(), '',
+         '### rows are checks. v5 read back by b710`s parser (tools/b710_standing.py parse): %d rows ; v4 %d rows. The rows cut from relay data/b716_ferry.txt :9 and :17.' % (len(r2), n0),
+         '### The NEG control: a copy of v5 with delta (ii)`s "%s" altered to "%s", its row compared again.' % (NEG_FROM, NEG_TO), '',
+         '  v5`s rows (1)-(%d) equal to v4`s row for row (number, kind, separator, tag, clause, source line): %s' % (n0, first_equal),
+         '  v4`s body lines (its LINE sections and rows) present in v5 line for line: %s' % v1_lines_equal]
+    L += ['  (%d) :%d %s: equal to its span of the ferry %s ; its control within it %s ; %d characters' % (
+        d['n'], d['line'], 'the governing row' if d['row'] == 'governing' else ('delta (%s)' % d['row'] if d['line'] == 17 else d['row']), d['equal'],
+        d['control_in'] if (d['line'] == 17 and d['row'] != 'governing') else 'n/a (not a delta)', d['chars']) for d in dres]
+    L += ['  (%d) the close`s order at v5: equal to the tool`s ORDER (%d steps) %s' % (n0 + len(flat) + 1, len(ORDER), order_equal),
+          '  NEG: the altered copy`s delta (ii) equal to the ferry %s (wanted False ; the phrase present in v5 %s)' % (neg_equal, NEG_FROM in v2),
+          '  VERSION %s (wanted %d)' % (ver.group(1) if ver else 'NONE', VERSION), '',
+          '### ### **ROWS %d ; v4`S %d UNCHANGED %s ; ROWS CUT %d EQUAL %s ; THE ORDER ROW %s ; THE NEG COPY DIFFERS %s ; VERSION 5 %s ; THE CONTROL AS WANTED %s.**' % (
+              len(r2), n0, first_equal and v1_lines_equal, len(dres), all(d['equal'] for d in dres), order_equal, not neg_equal, bool(ver) and ver.group(1) == str(VERSION), ok)]
+    if 'dry' not in a:
+        for name, data in (('b716_standing_control.txt', NL.join(L) + NL),
+                           ('b716_standing_control.json', json.dumps(dict(at=utc(), rows=dres, first_equal=first_equal, v1_lines_equal=v1_lines_equal, neg_equal=neg_equal,
+                                                                          order_equal=order_equal, n=len(r2), ok=ok), indent=1) + NL)):
+            p = os.path.join(D, name)
+            _membrane(p)
+            with open(p + '.tmp', 'wb') as fh:
+                fh.write(data.encode('utf-8'))
+            os.replace(p + '.tmp', p)
+    print(NL.join(L[5:]))
+    if not ok:
+        sys.exit(1)
+
+
+if __name__ == '__main__':
+    fn = {'write': write, 'control': control}.get(sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else '')
+    if fn is None:
+        print('usage: b716_standing.py write [dry] | control [dry]')
+        sys.exit(2)
+    fn(*sys.argv[2:])

+# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
+r"""b716_tests.py -- EVERY TEST FILE UNDER tools/, RUN AT STEP ZERO AND COUNTED, under (R238)(2), standing's standing line (every act runs
+every test file under tools/ at step zero and prints the counts, so a failing case is found by the act after the edit that broke it).
+Carried from b715_tests.py at b716 under (R326)'s ferry, written whole by the Write tool: the lists in force are b704's zeta list (the faces
+page's :3 names it since b704's regeneration, PLACE-papers c9b2e55; b705 to b715 wrote no page) and b651's chi list with b704's and b643's
+probes; the bank is data/b716_tests_<stage>.*; a test withheld is read from data/b716_tests_withheld.json (no lean run on this host); each
+python test started as `python -X utf8`, no environment mapping passed; one process per test in the FOREGROUND under the 600 s limit. `parts`
+gives every file whose b715 run exceeded 450 s a part of its own (data/b684_rules.txt row (1) applied to the test files); `mem` prints the
+host's free memory, run before each part starts; `hold` prints the free memory against the 2,560 MB hold and exits 3 where it stands beneath
+it, so a part is not run and the figure is printed.
+
+### `run <test> [stage]`: one test per call, its cases counted by the numbered case pattern ((R233)(3), `^  \(\d+\) ` ending PASS or FAIL)
+### and, where a test prints no numbered case, its exit code and last line read instead; the result appended to data/b716_tests_<stage>.json.
+### `report [stage]`: data/b716_tests_<stage>.txt from it. `parts`, `mem`, `hold`: print only.
+"""
+import ctypes
+import io
+import json
+import os
+import re
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import time
+
+ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
+D, T = os.path.join(ROOT, 'data'), os.path.join(ROOT, 'tools')
+NL = chr(10)
+PP = 'D:/MY-DOwnloads/PLACE-papers'
+SP = 'C:/Users/ECHOCH~1/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/D--/e594f88a-2fab-4757-943d-7ab1bc3de815/scratchpad'   # ### b643: b637's planted directory copied here whole (B637Planted.lean sha256 735629f5...)
+COUNT_CASE = r'^  \(\d+\) '
+if hasattr(sys.stdout, 'reconfigure'):
+    sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+
+ZL, ZP = os.path.join(D, 'b704_nodes_zeta.txt'), os.path.join(D, 'b704_probe_out_zeta.txt')   # ### the list the page names at :3 since b704
+ZPAGE = os.path.join(PP, 'THE_CLAUSE_AND_ITS_COMPILED_FACES.md')
+CL, CPR = os.path.join(D, 'b651_nodes_chi.txt'), os.path.join(D, 'b643_probe_out_chi.txt')
+ARGS = {'test_chain_page.py': [ZL, ZP, ZPAGE, CL, CPR], 'test_g_chain_page.py': [ZL, ZP, ZPAGE],
+        'test_e0_rule.py': [SP + '/b637_planted']}
+LIMIT = {'test_elab_reader_b634.py': 3000, 'test_asof.py': 580, 'test_chain_page.py': 580}
+BUDGET = 300               # ### a part planned at half the limit (b682 defect (b): a part ran 27% over its plan at low memory)
+ALONE_ABOVE = 450           # ### data/b684_rules.txt row (1), applied to the test files
+PRIOR = 'b715_tests_stepzero.json'
+HOLD = 2560                 # ### the host memory hold, the author's word on every host ((R294)(2))
+ACT = 'b716'
+
+
+def bank(stage):
+    return os.path.join(D, '%s_tests_%s.json' % (ACT, stage))
+
+
+def tests():
+    return sorted(f for f in os.listdir(T) if f.startswith('test_') and (f.endswith('.py') or f.endswith('.sh')))
+
+
+def load(stage):
+    try:
+        return json.load(io.open(bank(stage), encoding='utf-8'))
+    except Exception:
+        return {}
+
+
+def _membrane(p):
+    """(R326)(2)(ii), standing clauses v2 row (53): the membrane before a write under relay data/ (tools/act_membrane.py guard_path)."""
+    import act_membrane as MEM
+    import b716_record as REC
+    MEM.guard_path(ACT, p, REC.R.ROOT_EXCLUDE)
+
+
+def save(stage, j):
+    b = (json.dumps(j, indent=1, ensure_ascii=False) + NL).encode('utf-8')
+    _membrane(bank(stage))
+    with open(bank(stage) + '.tmp', 'wb') as fh:
+        fh.write(b)
+    os.replace(bank(stage) + '.tmp', bank(stage))
+
+
+def withheld():
+    try:
+        return json.load(io.open(os.path.join(D, '%s_tests_withheld.json' % ACT), encoding='utf-8'))
+    except Exception:
+        return {}
+
+
+def free_mb():
+    class MS(ctypes.Structure):
+        _fields_ = [('dwLength', ctypes.c_ulong), ('dwMemoryLoad', ctypes.c_ulong), ('ullTotalPhys', ctypes.c_ulonglong),
+                    ('ullAvailPhys', ctypes.c_ulonglong), ('ullTotalPageFile', ctypes.c_ulonglong), ('ullAvailPageFile', ctypes.c_ulonglong),
+                    ('ullTotalVirtual', ctypes.c_ulonglong), ('ullAvailVirtual', ctypes.c_ulonglong), ('ullAvailExtendedVirtual', ctypes.c_ulonglong)]
+    m = MS()
+    m.dwLength = ctypes.sizeof(MS)
+    ctypes.windll.kernel32.GlobalMemoryStatusEx(ctypes.byref(m))
+    return int(m.ullAvailPhys // (1024 * 1024)), int(m.ullTotalPhys // (1024 * 1024))
+
+
+def mem(*a):
+    f, t = free_mb()
+    print('%s  ### THE HOST`S FREE MEMORY %d MB of %d MB%s' % (time.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ', time.gmtime()), f, t,
+                                                             (' -- before part %s' % a[0]) if a else ''), flush=True)
+    return f
+
+
+def hold(*a):
+    f = mem(*a)
+    if f < HOLD:
+        print('%s  ### BENEATH THE HOLD %d MB: THE PART IS NOT RUN ((R294)(2))' % (time.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ', time.gmtime()), HOLD), flush=True)
+        sys.exit(3)
+    print('  ### at or above the hold %d MB: the part may start' % HOLD, flush=True)
+
+
+def run(name, stage):
+    if name in withheld():
+        print('%s  %-34s ### WITHHELD: %s' % (time.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ', time.gmtime()), name, withheld()[name]), flush=True)
+        return
+    cmd = (['bash', os.path.join(T, name)] if name.endswith('.sh') else [sys.executable, '-X', 'utf8', os.path.join(T, name)]) + ARGS.get(name, [])
+    t0 = time.time()
+    try:
+        r = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, encoding='utf-8', errors='replace', cwd=ROOT, timeout=LIMIT.get(name, 580))
+        rc, out = r.returncode, (r.stdout or '') + (r.stderr or '')
+    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as e:
+        rc, out = -9, ((e.stdout or b'').decode('utf-8', 'replace') if isinstance(e.stdout, bytes) else (e.stdout or '')) + NL + '### TIMED OUT AT %d s' % LIMIT.get(name, 580)
+    cases = [l for l in out.split(NL) if re.match(COUNT_CASE, l)]
+    passing = sum(1 for c in cases if c.rstrip().endswith('PASS'))
+    failing = [re.match(r'^  (\(\d+\))', c).group(1) for c in cases if not c.rstrip().endswith('PASS')]
+    last = [l for l in out.split(NL) if l.strip()][-1:] or ['']
+    j = load(stage)
+    j[name] = dict(rc=rc, seconds=int(time.time() - t0), cases=len(cases), passing=passing, failing=failing,
+                   last=last[0][:200], args=[os.path.basename(a) for a in ARGS.get(name, [])], output=out, free_mb_after=free_mb()[0])
+    save(stage, j)
+    print('%s  %-34s exit %d ; cases %d ; passing %d ; failing %s ; %d s ; last: %s' % (
+        time.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ', time.gmtime()), name, rc, len(cases), passing, failing or 'NONE', j[name]['seconds'], last[0][:90]), flush=True)
+
+
+def parts(*a):
+    """the tests partitioned by b715's recorded seconds (relay data/b715_tests_stepzero.json): a file above ALONE_ABOVE its own part; the rest
+    in parts each planned under BUDGET."""
+    prior = {}
+    try:
+        prior = dict((k, v.get('seconds', 60)) for k, v in json.load(io.open(os.path.join(D, PRIOR), encoding='utf-8')).items())
+    except Exception:
+        pass
+    out, cur, s = [], [], 0
+    for n in tests():
+        if n in withheld():
+            continue
+        t = prior.get(n, 60)
+        if t > ALONE_ABOVE:
+            out.append(([n], t))
+            continue
+        if cur and s + t > BUDGET:
+            out.append((cur, s))
+            cur, s = [], 0
+        cur.append(n)
+        s += t
+    if cur:
+        out.append((cur, s))
+    for i, (ns, s) in enumerate(out, 1):
+        print('PART %d (%d s planned%s): %s' % (i, s, ', ALONE' if len(ns) == 1 and s > ALONE_ABOVE else '', ' '.join(ns)))
+
+
+def report(stage):
+    j = load(stage)
+    L = ['%s -- %s: EVERY TEST FILE UNDER tools/, RUN AND COUNTED ((R238)(2), standing) (%s)' % (
+        ACT, 'STEP ZERO' if stage == 'stepzero' else stage.upper(), time.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ', time.gmtime())), '']
+    wh = withheld()
+    for n in tests():
+        x = j.get(n)
+        if x is None and n in wh:
+            L.append('  %-34s ### WITHHELD: %s' % (n, wh[n]))
+            continue
+        if x is None:
+            L.append('  %-34s ### NOT RUN' % n)
+            continue
+        how = ('numbered cases %d, passing %d, failing %s ; its own last line: %s' % (x['cases'], x['passing'], x['failing'] or 'none', x['last'][:90])
+               if x['cases'] else 'no numbered case printed; exit and last line read: %s' % x['last'][:120])
+        L.append('  %-34s exit %d ; %s ; %d s%s' % (n, x['rc'], how, x['seconds'], (' ; args ' + ' '.join(x['args'])) if x['args'] else ''))
+    rs = subprocess.run(['git', '-C', ROOT, 'status', '--porcelain', '--untracked-files=no'], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.strip()
+    ps = subprocess.run(['git', '-C', PP, 'status', '--porcelain', '--untracked-files=no'], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.strip()
+    nf = [n for n in tests() if n in j and (j[n]['rc'] != 0 or j[n]['failing'])]
+    longest = max((j[n]['seconds'] for n in tests() if n in j), default=0)
+    L += ['', '### the trees after the runs: relay tracked changes %s ; PLACE-papers tracked changes %s' % (rs.replace(NL, ', ') or 'NONE', ps.replace(NL, ', ') or 'NONE'),
+          '### the longest test %d s (the 600 s limit); tests over the 590 s guard, whose part the harness moved to the background: %s' % (
+              longest, ' '.join('%s %d s' % (n, j[n]['seconds']) for n in tests() if n in j and j[n]['seconds'] > 590) or 'NONE'),
+          '', '### ### **TEST FILES %d ; RUN %d ; WITHHELD %d ; NOT CLEAN %d %s.**' % (len(tests()), sum(n in j for n in tests()),
+                                                                                     sum(n in wh and n not in j for n in tests()), len(nf),
+                                                                                     ['%s %s' % (n, j[n]['failing'] or 'exit %d' % j[n]['rc']) for n in nf] or '')]
+    L += ['', '### THE OUTPUTS, WHOLE:']
+    for n in tests():
+        if n in j:
+            L += ['', '=== %s' % n] + j[n]['output'].rstrip(NL).split(NL)
+    b = (NL.join(L) + NL).encode('utf-8')
+    p = os.path.join(D, '%s_tests_%s.txt' % (ACT, stage))
+    _membrane(p)
+    with open(p + '.tmp', 'wb') as fh:
+        fh.write(b)
+    os.replace(p + '.tmp', p)
+    print(NL.join(L[2:2 + len(tests()) + 5]))
+
+
+if __name__ == '__main__':
+    if len(sys.argv) > 2 and sys.argv[1] == 'run':
+        run(sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3] if len(sys.argv) > 3 else 'stepzero')
+    elif len(sys.argv) > 1 and sys.argv[1] == 'report':
+        report(sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 'stepzero')
+    elif len(sys.argv) > 1 and sys.argv[1] == 'parts':
+        parts()
+    elif len(sys.argv) > 1 and sys.argv[1] == 'mem':
+        mem(*sys.argv[2:])
+    elif len(sys.argv) > 1 and sys.argv[1] == 'hold':
+        hold(*sys.argv[2:])
+    else:
+        print('usage: b716_tests.py run <test> [stage] | report [stage] | parts | mem [part] | hold [part]')
+        sys.exit(2)

+def orders_naming(ot_text, act):
+    """(R326)(3), the NEXT line's repair: every open-orders row of OPEN_TRAILS whose status names <act> -- a row is a line opening
+    `*Appended` that carries `THE ORDER **`W-ORD-`; its status is its text from its last `Status`; returned as (line, order, status). Pure in
+    its arguments (b711's port clause was made b715's after the seal and stood outside the sealed NEXT, events :46)."""
+    out = []
+    for i, l in enumerate((ot_text or '').replace(chr(13), '').split(NL), 1):
+        m = re.search(r'THE ORDER \*\*`(W-ORD-[A-Z0-9-]+)`', l)
+        if not (l.startswith('*Appended') and m):
+            continue
+        k = l.rfind('Status')
+        st = l[k:] if k >= 0 else ''
+        if re.search(r'\b%s\b' % re.escape(act), st):
+            out.append((i, m.group(1), st.strip()))
+    return out
+
+
+def next_line(pp=PP, act=NEXT_ACT, text=None):
+    """the NEXT line read at closing time: the ruling's named items (NEXT), then every open-orders row whose status names the next act, read
+    from OPEN_TRAILS now (or from <text>, for a control)."""
+    if text is None:
+        p = os.path.join(pp, 'OPEN_TRAILS.md')
+        text = io.open(p, encoding='utf-8', errors='replace').read() if os.path.exists(p) else ''
+    rows = orders_naming(text, act)
+    return NEXT + '; the open orders whose status names %s (OPEN_TRAILS, read at closing time): %s' % (
+        act, '; '.join('%s (:%d, %s)' % (o, i, s[:160]) for i, o, s in rows) or 'NONE')

+# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
+"""standing/allow.py -- AN ARM READS ITS ALLOWANCES FROM THE RULING ((R326)(3)(i) of the registrations act; events (7) and (8) of the
+toolset act): the branches an act may delete and the prompts it may put are read from the act's ruling (data/<act>_ferry.txt) and its
+author's-words banks, not from floors in an arm's code.
+
+The deletes: every clause of the ruling (a sentence or a semicolon's part) that says a branch is deleted, and does not say NOT deleted,
+names its branches; a delete is allowed when its branch's name is among them. A word of the author's that names a deletion adds its
+branches the same way. Provenance: the toolset act's G-DELETES-ONE-BRANCH admitted a fixed tuple of names (its NAMED_PUSH), and its two
+push branches deleted after the entry's and the pre-root pushes failed it.
+The prompts: the ruling's "root's prompt put once" allows one root prompt; every other prompt is allowed when the author's word that
+answers it is banked under its "## (n)" head (data/<act>_author_words_preseal.txt, data/<act>_author_words.txt), the k-th prompt by the
+k-th head. Provenance: the toolset act's G-ANSWERS-BEFORE-WRITES wanted FLOORS['prompts'] = 1 PROMPT row where the act put four.
+
+  deletes_allowed(ruling, words) -> [names]        prompts_read(answers, ruling, heads) -> dict(rows, allowed, unbanked, ok)
+  deletes_read(deleted, ruling, words) -> dict(rows, outside, ok)
+"""
+import os
+import re
+import sys
+
+sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
+import act as A   # noqa: E402
+
+BRANCH = re.compile(r'(?<![\w/.-])((?:push|recut|port|merge)-[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9.-]*[A-Za-z0-9])')
+NOT_DELETED = re.compile(r'\bNOT\s+deleted\b|\bnot\s+deleted\b|\bnot\s+be\s+deleted\b')
+DELETED = re.compile(r'\bdeleted?\b', re.I)
+ROOT_ONCE = re.compile(r'root[’\']s prompt put once', re.I)
+PROMPT_ROW = re.compile(r'^### PROMPT \d+ \(([^)]*)\):', re.M)
+HEAD = re.compile(r'^## \((\d+)\) ', re.M)
+
+
+def clauses(text):
+    """the ruling's text cut at sentence ends and semicolons, each part with its whitespace collapsed."""
+    flat = re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', text or '')
+    return [c.strip() for c in re.split(r'(?<=[.;])\s+(?=[A-Za-z(])', flat) if c.strip()]
+
+
+def deletes_allowed(ruling, words=''):
+    """the branch names the ruling's delete clauses and the author's words name, in order, each once."""
+    out = []
+    for c in clauses(ruling) + clauses(words):
+        if DELETED.search(c) and not NOT_DELETED.search(c):
+            for b in BRANCH.findall(c):
+                if b not in out:
+                    out.append(b)
+    return out
+
+
+def deletes_kept(ruling, words=''):
+    """the branch names a clause says are NOT deleted."""
+    out = []
+    for c in clauses(ruling) + clauses(words):
+        if NOT_DELETED.search(c):
+            out += [b for b in BRANCH.findall(c) if b not in out]
+    return out
+
+
+def deletes_read(deleted, ruling, words=''):
+    """each delete [(repository, branch)] read against the allowance: allowed where its name is among the ruling's delete names and not
+    among the kept. Returns dict(rows [(repo, branch, allowed)], outside [(repo, branch)], allowed names, ok)."""
+    allowed, kept = deletes_allowed(ruling, words), deletes_kept(ruling, words)
+    rows = [(r, b, b in allowed and b not in kept) for r, b in deleted]
+    outside = [(r, b) for r, b, ok in rows if not ok]
+    return dict(rows=rows, outside=outside, allowed=allowed, kept=kept, ok=not outside)
+
+
+def prompts_read(answers, ruling, heads_text):
+    """each PROMPT row of an answers bank read against the allowance: a root prompt against the ruling's "put once" (one allowed), every
+    other prompt against the author's-words heads, the k-th by the k-th. Returns dict(rows [(label, kind, allowed)], allowed, unbanked, ok)."""
+    labels = PROMPT_ROW.findall(answers or '')
+    root_allowed = 1 if ROOT_ONCE.search(re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', ruling or '')) else 0
+    heads = len(HEAD.findall(heads_text or ''))
+    rows, roots, others = [], 0, 0
+    for lab in labels:
+        if lab.strip().lower().startswith('root'):
+            roots += 1
+            rows.append((lab, 'root', roots <= root_allowed))
+        else:
+            others += 1
+            rows.append((lab, 'word', others <= heads))
+    unbanked = [lab for lab, _k, ok in rows if not ok]
+    return dict(rows=rows, allowed=root_allowed + heads, unbanked=unbanked, ok=not unbanked)
+
+
+def ruling_of(act):
+    return A.rd('%s_ferry.txt' % act)
+
+
+def words_of(act):
+    """the author's-words banks of an act, joined: the pre-seal bank and the post-seal bank, each by its "## (n)" heads."""
+    return A.rd('%s_author_words_preseal.txt' % act) + '\n' + A.rd('%s_author_words.txt' % act)
+

+# ======================================================================== THE SEAL COMMITS WHAT IT HASHES (the class repair (3)(ii))
+def seal_guard(tools, rev='HEAD', read=None, at_rev=None):
+    """the seal's guard before it hashes: each sealed tool (a path under relay tools/) whose bytes in the working tree differ from its blob
+    at `rev` (the commit the seal will record, HEAD) is refused -- a tool edited after its last commit is committed in the seal's own step
+    before the seal hashes, so the head the seal records carries every byte it hashed. `read` (path -> bytes) and `at_rev` (path -> bytes
+    or None) may be passed for a constructed control. Returns dict(ok, refused [(tool, reason)], head)."""
+    def _read(t):
+        p = os.path.join(A.T, *t.split('/'))
+        if not os.path.isfile(p):
+            return None
+        with open(p, 'rb') as f:
+            return f.read()
+    read = read or _read
+    at_rev = at_rev or (lambda t: A.blob(rev, 'tools/' + t))
+    refused = []
+    for t in tools:
+        w, c = read(t), at_rev(t)
+        if w is None:
+            refused.append((t, 'absent in the working tree'))
+        elif c is None:
+            refused.append((t, 'not committed at %s' % rev))
+        elif A.sha256_bytes(w) != A.sha256_bytes(c):
+            refused.append((t, 'edited after its last commit: working %s, at %s %s' % (A.sha256_bytes(w)[:16], rev, A.sha256_bytes(c)[:16])))
+    head = A.gs('relay', 'rev-parse', rev) if not refused else None
+    return dict(ok=not refused, refused=refused, head=head)
+
+
+def seal_head(hashes, commits):
+    """the first commit of `commits` (in order) whose blobs carry every sealed tool's sha256 in `hashes` ({tool: sha256}): the head a seal
+    that commits what it hashes records. None where no commit carries them all."""
+    for c in commits:
+        if all((lambda b: b is not None and A.sha256_bytes(b) == h)(A.blob(c, 'tools/' + t)) for t, h in hashes.items()):
+            return c
+    return None
+
+
+def sealed_list(act, names=None):
+    """the tools an act's seal hashes, by rule: tools/<act>_*.py but the act's report tool (sealed only after the root's word), and every
+    file of tools/standing/. `names` (paths relative to tools/) may be passed, for the replay of a seal at a commit."""
+    if names is None:
+        names = [f for f in os.listdir(A.T) if f.endswith('.py')] + ['standing/' + f for f in os.listdir(os.path.join(A.T, 'standing'))
+                                                                    if f.endswith('.py') or f == 'VERSION']
+    own = [n for n in names if re.match(r'^%s_\w+\.py$' % re.escape(act), n) and n != '%s_report.py' % act]
+    std = [n for n in names if re.match(r'^standing/(\w+\.py|VERSION)$', n)]
+    return sorted(own + std)
+
+
+def lock_of(act, date):
+    """the lock's time on the act's registration face (data/<act>_registration_<date>.txt, its "locked at (UTC)" line), or None."""
+    m = re.search(r'locked at \(UTC\)\s*:\s*(\d{4}-\d\d-\d\dT\d\d:\d\d:\d\dZ)', A.rd('%s_registration_%s.txt' % (act, date)))
+    return m.group(1) if m else None
+
+
+def seal_write(act, parts, date, withheld_spec, prev=None, write=True):
+    """step [8], the seal: refused where the face is not locked, the record exists, a sealed tool is edited after its last commit (the
+    guard), an arm is without its NEG control shown failing, or a part's run on the previous act's banks is not banked; else the record
+    written once, data/<act>_seal_hashes.json, its relay head the guard's (HEAD, which carries every byte hashed). Returns (code, lines)."""
+    L, p = [], os.path.join(A.D, '%s_seal_hashes.json' % act)
+    lock = lock_of(act, date)
+    if lock is None:
+        return 3, ['### THE FACE IS NOT LOCKED -- NOTHING RECORDED']
+    if os.path.exists(p):
+        return 3, ['### THE HASHES ARE RECORDED ALREADY -- NOTHING RECORDED TWICE']
+    tools = sealed_list(act)
+    g = seal_guard(tools)
+    if not g['ok']:
+        return 3, ['### THE SEAL`S GUARD: %d SEALED TOOL(S) NOT AS COMMITTED AT HEAD -- COMMIT THEM IN THE SEAL`S STEP; NOTHING RECORDED' % len(g['refused'])] + [
+            '  %-38s %s' % (t, w) for t, w in g['refused']]
+    banks = exercise_banks(act, parts)
+    arms = arms_of(banks, parts)
+    ns = neg_shown(arms, banks, withheld_entries(withheld_spec))
+    aparts = sorted(set(q for _n, q in arms))
+    missing = [q for q in aparts if A.jl('%s_prevrun_exercise_%s.json' % (act, q)).get('mode') != 'prev']
+    L += ['  WITHHELD, NOT EXERCISABLE: %-46s %s' % (n, w) for n, w in ns.withheld]
+    if ns.bad or missing:
+        return 3, L + ['### %d OF %d ARMS WITHOUT THEIR NEG CONTROL SHOWN FAILING ON A MUTATED COPY IN THE PRE-RUN:' % (len(ns.bad), len(arms))] + [
+            '  %-46s %s' % (n, w) for n, w in ns.bad] + ['### THE PARTS WHOSE ARMS ARE NOT RUN ON %s`S BANKS BEFORE THE SEAL: %s' % (prev, missing or 'NONE'),
+                                                         '### ### **THE SEAL REFUSED -- NOTHING RECORDED.**']
+    dtext = A.rd('%s_defects.txt' % act)
+    now = dict((t, A.sha256_file(os.path.join(A.T, *t.split('/')))) for t in tools)
+    prev_run = dict((q, dict((k, len(v)) for k, v in A.jl('%s_prevrun_exercise_%s.json' % (act, q)).items() if k in PREV_KEYS)) for q in aparts)
+    j = dict(at=time.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ', time.gmtime()), lock=lock, tools=now, relay_head=g['head'], letters=letters_of(dtext),
+             defects_sha256=A.sha256_bytes(dtext.encode('utf-8')), neg_shown=len(ns.shown), withheld=[dict(arm=n, reason=w) for n, w in ns.withheld],
+             prev_run=prev_run)
+    if write:
+        with open(p + '.tmp', 'wb') as fh:
+            fh.write((json.dumps(j, indent=1, ensure_ascii=False) + NL).encode('utf-8'))
+        os.replace(p + '.tmp', p)
+    L += ['  %-38s %s' % (t, now[t]) for t in tools]
+    L += ['  the letters entered before the seal %d: %s' % (len(j['letters']), ', '.join(s[:3] for s in j['letters']) or 'NONE'),
+          '  the arms with their NEG control shown failing %d of %d ; withheld, not exercisable, %d (%s) ; the runs on %s`s banks %s' % (
+              len(ns.shown), len(arms), len(ns.withheld), ', '.join(n for n, _w in ns.withheld) or 'NONE', prev, prev_run),
+          '  the relay head recorded %s (the guard`s: every sealed byte committed there)' % g['head'],
+          '  %s: %s' % ('written' if write else 'dry, not written', os.path.basename(p))]
+    return 0, L
+
+
+def main(route):
+    """`seal` writes the act's seal record (step [8]); `dry` runs every refusal and prints, writing nothing."""
+    cur = A.current()
+    code, L = seal_write(cur['act'], cur['parts'], cur['date'], cur.get('withheld'), cur.get('prev'), write=(route == 'seal'))
+    print(NL.join(L))
+    return code
+

+if __name__ == '__main__':
+    sys.exit(main(sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 'dry'))

+def _section(text, head, stop=r'^## '):
+    """the lines of a bank's section from its `head` line to the next `stop` line."""
+    L = (text or '').split(NL)
+    out, on = [], False
+    for l in L:
+        if l.startswith(head):
+            on = True
+            continue
+        if on and re.match(stop, l):
+            break
+        if on:
+            out.append(l)
+    return out
+
+
+def _paragraph(text, n):
+    """an events bank's item (n): its starting line to the next starting line, joined."""
+    out, on = [], False
+    for l in (text or '').split(NL):
+        m = re.match(r'^\((\d+)\) ', l)
+        if m:
+            on = int(m.group(1)) == n
+        if on:
+            out.append(l)
+    return ' '.join(out)
+
+
+def _required_of(src):
+    """REQUIRED_BANKS of an act's suite, read from its source by its assignment (not imported)."""
+    import ast
+    for node in ast.parse(src).body:
+        if isinstance(node, ast.Assign) and any(isinstance(t, ast.Name) and t.id == 'REQUIRED_BANKS' for t in node.targets):
+            return list(ast.literal_eval(node.value))
+    return []
+

### tools/standing/rootbanks.py : NEW, diff 37 lines, repair (3)(vi)

+# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
+r"""standing/rootbanks.py -- THE ROOT'S REQUIRED BANKS READ AGAINST ITS EXCLUSIONS ((R326)(3)(vi) of the registrations act; defect (j) of the
+toolset act): a bank an act's suite requires among the root's items, that the root route's exclusion takes out, is refused at the suite's
+import, before any part runs.
+
+The exclusion is the act's root route's own pattern over a bank's basename (the record tool's ROOT_EXCLUDE, passed in), or one built here
+from an act and its excluded kinds in the record tools' form, `^<act>_(<kinds>)\.?(txt|json|md|jsonl|)$`. Provenance: the per-act suites'
+import check since b706's (j) (`_EXCLUDED_REQUIRED`, e.g. tools/b715_checks.py :187-:189), made one standing function; the toolset act's
+carried kind `chain_.*` (b707's) read its required chain-rows bank as excluded until it was narrowed to `chain_half_.*`.
+
+  exclusion(act, kinds, names=()) -> compiled pattern      overlap(required, exclude) -> [paths]
+  guard(required, exclude) -> None, or SystemExit naming the overlap
+"""
+import os
+import re
+
+
+def exclusion(act, kinds, names=()):
+    """the root route's exclusion in the record tools' form, from an act, its excluded kinds (a regex alternation) and named banks."""
+    return re.compile(r'^' + re.escape(act) + r'_(' + kinds + (('|' + '|'.join(names)) if names else '') + r')\.?(txt|json|md|jsonl|)$')
+
+
+def overlap(required, exclude):
+    """the required banks (relay paths) whose basename the exclusion takes out."""
+    return [p for p in required if exclude.match(os.path.basename(p))]
+
+
+def guard(required, exclude):
+    """refuse at import: a SystemExit naming every required bank the exclusion takes out; nothing where there is none."""
+    over = overlap(required, exclude)
+    if over:
+        raise SystemExit('### THE ROOT`S REQUIRED BANKS NAME %d BANK(S) ITS EXCLUSION TAKES OUT (the class repair (3)(vi)): %s ; the exclusion %s' % (
+            len(over), over, exclude.pattern))
+

     return [p for p in out.split(NL) if p.strip()]
+
+
+def roster_at(rev='main'):
+    """the roster the builder reads, from the base roster's families and PLACE-papers' tree at `rev` (the class repair (3)(iii): the
+    mirror's verifier reads this roster, at the source head the build's MANIFEST declares, not the base roster alone)."""
+    return resolve(base(), main_tree(rev))[0]
+
+
+def build_joins(act):
+    """the joins an act's mirror build printed (data/<act>_mirror_build.json `out`, its JOINS rows) and the source head it built from."""
+    j = A.jl('%s_mirror_build.json' % act)
+    return re.findall(r'^  JOINS (\S+)', j.get('out') or '', re.M), j.get('pp_head')

### tools/standing/words.py : NEW, diff 125 lines, repair (3)(iv), (3)(v)
--- tools/standing/words.py at cf51ee95

@@ -0,0 +1,122 @@
+# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
+"""standing/words.py -- THE AUTHOR'S WORDS, READ BY THEIR HEADS ((R326)(3)(iv)-(v) of the registrations act; events (3), (4) and (6) of the
+toolset act).
+
+(iv) THE ANSWERS ROUTE READS EVERY WORD. An answers bank's RESULT rows (each from its "RESULT (transcript line N, <time>):" line to the
+harness's tail that closes the answer) carry the author's word between BEGIN and END wherever it stands -- after the answer's `="`, or inside an option's notes
+after a preview, as the toolset act's word (2) arrived. Each word is written as a "## (n)" head with its time and transcript line, the word
+verbatim beneath it, and the words are read back by those heads alone; a line opening "## (" that is not a well-formed head in sequence is
+refused with its line. Provenance: tools/b698_record.py's answers route (:321) matched a word at the answer's start or after `="` and read
+three of the toolset act's four.
+(v) A WORD AFTER A SEALED COMPOSITION. A clause a sealed tool composed (the entry's, the trail's) is printed beside every word received
+after the composition's time, read from the words' heads; a word received before it is not printed. Provenance: the toolset act's entry
+and trail landed clauses its sealed record tool composed before the root word that ruled them.
+
+  results(answers) -> [dict(line, transcript, at, text)]     word_of(text) -> str or None
+  heads_text(words) -> str                                   read_heads(text) -> dict(words [dict(n, at, transcript, word)], refused)
+  after(words, at) -> [word]                                 beside(clauses, words, at) -> [str]
+"""
+import re
+
+RESULT = re.compile(r'^RESULT \(transcript line (\d+), ([0-9T:.\-]+Z)\):')
+HEAD_LINE = re.compile(r'^## \((\d+)\) (\S+Z), transcript line (\d+)\s*$')
+HEAD_START = re.compile(r'^## \(')
+TAIL = ' Read the answers carefully'
+
+
+def results(answers):
+    """the RESULT rows of an answers bank, each with its own line, its transcript line, its time and its text to the next '### ' row."""
+    L = (answers or '').replace('\r', '').split('\n')
+    out, i = [], 0
+    while i < len(L):
+        m = RESULT.match(L[i])
+        if not m:
+            i += 1
+            continue
+        # the row runs to the harness's tail, which closes every answer; a quoted question carries "### " lines of its own (the toolset
+        # act's root prompt quoted its FINDINGS line), so a "### " line ends the row only where no tail follows before the next RESULT
+        j = i
+        while j < len(L) and TAIL not in L[j] and not (j > i and RESULT.match(L[j])):
+            j += 1
+        if j < len(L) and TAIL in L[j]:
+            j += 1
+        else:
+            j = i + 1
+            while j < len(L) and not L[j].startswith('### ') and not RESULT.match(L[j]):
+                j += 1
+        out.append(dict(line=i + 1, transcript=int(m.group(1)), at=m.group(2), text='\n'.join(L[i:j]).rstrip()))
+        i = j
+    return out
+
+
+def word_of(text):
+    """the author's word in a RESULT's text: from its first BEGIN to the last END before the harness's tail, wherever the word stands."""
+    t = text or ''
+    b = t.find('BEGIN')
+    if b < 0:
+        return None
+    k = t.find(TAIL)
+    e = t.rfind('END', b + 5, k if k >= 0 else len(t))
+    return t[b + 5:e] if e > b else None
+
+
+def words(answers):
+    """[dict(at, transcript, word)] for every RESULT carrying a word."""
+    out = []
+    for r in results(answers):
+        w = word_of(r['text'])
+        if w is not None:
+            out.append(dict(at=r['at'], transcript=r['transcript'], word=w))
+    return out
+
+
+def heads_text(ws):
+    """the words written as "## (n) <time>, transcript line <N>" heads, each word verbatim beneath its head."""
+    L = []
+    for n, w in enumerate(ws, 1):
+        L += ['## (%d) %s, transcript line %d' % (n, w['at'], w['transcript']), w['word'], '']
+    return '\n'.join(L)
+
+
+def read_heads(text):
+    """the words read back by their heads alone: each "## (n)" head well formed and in sequence, its word the lines beneath it to the next
+    head. A line opening "## (" that is not a well-formed head, or a head out of sequence, is refused with its line."""
+    L = (text or '').replace('\r', '').split('\n')
+    ws, refused, cur = [], [], None
+    for i, l in enumerate(L, 1):
+        if HEAD_START.match(l):
+            m = HEAD_LINE.match(l)
+            if not m:
+                refused.append((i, l, 'not a well-formed head'))
+                cur = None
+                continue
+            if int(m.group(1)) != len(ws) + 1:
+                refused.append((i, l, 'out of sequence: (%d) after %d head(s)' % (int(m.group(1)), len(ws))))
+                cur = None
+                continue
+            cur = dict(n=int(m.group(1)), at=m.group(2), transcript=int(m.group(3)), word=[])
+            ws.append(cur)
+        elif cur is not None:
+            cur['word'].append(l)
+    for w in ws:
+        w['word'] = '\n'.join(w['word']).strip('\n')
+    return dict(words=ws, refused=refused)
+
+
+def after(ws, at):
+    """the words received after a composition's time `at` (ISO times compared to the second)."""
+    return [w for w in ws if w['at'][:19] > (at or '')[:19]]
+
+
+def beside(clauses, ws, at):
+    """each clause a sealed tool composed at `at`, printed with every word received after it beside it."""
+    late = after(ws, at)
+    out = []
+    for c in clauses:
+        out.append('  THE CLAUSE (composed %s): %s' % (at, c))
+        if not late:
+            out.append('      no word received after its composition')
+        for w in late:
+            out.append('      THE AUTHOR`S WORD RECEIVED AFTER IT (%s, transcript line %d): %s' % (w['at'], w['transcript'], w['word'][:300]))
+    return out
+

### ### **PAIRS 17 ; NOT FOUND 0  ; STANDING FILES 14 ; THE ACT`S CARRIED TOOLS EDITED 8 ; THE TOOLSET`S FILES CHANGED 10 (NEW 3).**
